<a id="words-1001-1100"></a>
## Words 1,001 – 1,100

<a id="w-1001"></a>
### 1001. [access](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/access_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈækses/` · **US:** `/ˈækses/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/acc/acces/access__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/acc/acces/access__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दाखिला, पैठ, प्रवेश
- **Examples:**
  - High-speed internet access has become a necessity.
  - access to something/somebody You need a password to get access to the computer system.
  - access to information/services/resources
  - access to healthcare/education

<a id="w-1002"></a>
### 1002. [acquire](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/acquire) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈkwaɪə(r)/` · **US:** `/əˈkwaɪər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/acq/acqui/acquire__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/acq/acqui/acquire__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्राप्त करना, लाना, अर्जित  करना
- **Examples:**
  - She has acquired a good knowledge of English.
  - How long will it take to acquire the necessary skills?
  - I would love to apply the newly acquired skills to a job that I enjoy.
  - He has acquired a reputation for dishonesty.

<a id="w-1003"></a>
### 1003. [adapt](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/adapt) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈdæpt/` · **US:** `/əˈdæpt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ada/adapt/adapt__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ada/adapt/adapt__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अनुकूल बनाना, के अनुरूप बनाना, बदलना
- **Examples:**
  - It's amazing how soon you adapt.
  - The organisms were forced to adapt in order to survive.
  - adapt to something Some animals have a remarkable ability to adapt to changing environments.
  - A large organization can be slow to adapt to change

<a id="w-1004"></a>
### 1004. [adjust](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/adjust) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈdʒʌst/` · **US:** `/əˈdʒʌst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/adj/adjus/adjust__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/adj/adjus/adjust__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बराबर करना, अनुकूल बनाना, ठीक करना
- **Examples:**
  - adjust something Watch out for sharp bends and adjust your speed accordingly.
  - This button is for adjusting the volume.
  - The brakes need to be adjusted.
  - adjust something to something Adjust your language to the age of your audience.

<a id="w-1005"></a>
### 1005. [admire](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/admire) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ədˈmaɪə(r)/` · **US:** `/ədˈmaɪər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/adm/admir/admire__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/adm/admir/admire__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समादर करना, प्रशंसा करना, सराहना करना
- **Examples:**
  - admire somebody/something I really admire your enthusiasm.
  - Her work was much admired by critics.
  - You have to admire the way he handled the situation.
  - Actually, I greatly admire and respect him.

<a id="w-1006"></a>
### 1006. [admit](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/admit) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ədˈmɪt/` · **US:** `/ədˈmɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/adm/admit/admit__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/adm/admit/admit__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्वीकार करना, अनुमति देना, अंदर आने की अनुमति देना
- **Examples:**
  - It was a stupid thing to do, I admit.
  - + speech ‘I'm very nervous,’ she admitted reluctantly.
  - admit to something Don't be afraid to admit to your mistakes.
  - admit to doing something She admits to being strict with her children.

<a id="w-1007"></a>
### 1007. [adopt](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/adopt) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈdɒpt/` · **US:** `/əˈdɑːpt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ado/adopt/adopt__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ado/adopt/adopt__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्वीकार करना, चुनना, मानना
- **Examples:**
  - a campaign to encourage childless couples to adopt
  - adopt somebody to adopt a child
  - She was forced to have her baby adopted.
  - She adopted three children from the orphanage.

<a id="w-1008"></a>
### 1008. [adult](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/adult_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/əˈdʌlt/` · **US:** `/ˈædʌlt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/adu/adult/adult__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/adu/adult/adult__us_2_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वयस्क, पूर्णविकसित  प्राणि, बालिग
- **Examples:**
  - preparing young people for adult life
  - He was a healthy young adult male.
  - the adult population
  - adult monkeys

<a id="w-1009"></a>
### 1009. [advance](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/advance_3) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ədˈvɑːns/` · **US:** `/ədˈvæns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/adv/advan/advance__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/adv/advan/advance__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अग्रसर होना, आगे बढना, प्रयास
- **Examples:**
  - Please give us advance warning of any changes.
  - We need advance notice of the numbers involved.
  - No advance booking is necessary on most departures.

<a id="w-1010"></a>
### 1010. [advice](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/advice) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ədˈvaɪs/` · **US:** `/ədˈvaɪs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/adv/advic/advice__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/adv/advic/advice__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** परामर्श, जानकार्, उपदेश/सलाह
- **Examples:**
  - expert/practical/professional/medical advice
  - We were advised to seek legal advice.
  - advice on something The service offers information and advice on possible careers.
  - Ask your teacher's advice on how to prepare for the exam.

<a id="w-1011"></a>
### 1011. [advise](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/advise) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ədˈvaɪz/` · **US:** `/ədˈvaɪz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/adv/advis/advise__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/adv/advis/advise__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सूचित करना, सूचना देना, सुझाव देना
- **Examples:**
  - advise against doing something I would strongly advise against going out on your own.
  - advise somebody against doing something They advised me against visiting the troubled south of the country.
  - advise somebody against something The Foreign Office is advising Britons against all non-essential travel to the region.
  - advise against something They advised against the use of military force.

<a id="w-1012"></a>
### 1012. [afford](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/afford) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈfɔːd/` · **US:** `/əˈfɔːrd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/aff/affor/afford__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/aff/affor/afford__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** देना, जुटा पाना{धन}, ख़र्च करना
- **Examples:**
  - afford something Can we afford a new car?
  - None of them could afford £50 for a ticket.
  - I’d give up work if I could afford it.
  - afford to do something We can't afford to go abroad this summer.

<a id="w-1013"></a>
### 1013. [ahead](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/ahead) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈhed/` · **US:** `/əˈhed/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ahe/ahead/ahead__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ahe/ahead/ahead__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आगे, पहले से, पहले
- **Examples:**
  - I'll run ahead and warn them.
  - The road ahead was blocked.
  - We've got a lot of hard work ahead.
  - This will create problems in the months ahead.

<a id="w-1014"></a>
### 1014. [aim](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/aim_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/eɪm/` · **US:** `/eɪm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/aim/aim__/aim__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/aim/aim__/aim__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लक्ष्य, ध्येय
- **Examples:**
  - the stated aims of the study
  - Our main aim is to provide affordable childcare.
  - His sole aim in life is to enjoy himself.
  - Teamwork is required in order to achieve these aims.

<a id="w-1015"></a>
### 1015. [aircraft](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/aircraft) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈeəkrɑːft/` · **US:** `/ˈerkræft/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/air/aircr/aircraft__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/air/aircr/aircraft__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विमान या वायुयान
- **Examples:**
  - fighter/transport/military aircraft
  - The aircraft was flown by a young American pilot.
  - We operate a fleet of six aircraft.
  - The systems are built for jet aircraft.

<a id="w-1016"></a>
### 1016. [alarm](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/alarm_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/əˈlɑːm/` · **US:** `/əˈlɑːrm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ala/alarm/alarm__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ala/alarm/alarm__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** घंटी, खतरे का  संकेत, संकट घंटी
- **Examples:**
  - She decided to sound the alarm (= warn people that the situation was dangerous).
  - I hammered on all the doors to raise the alarm.
  - By the time the alarm was raised the intruders had escaped.

<a id="w-1017"></a>
### 1017. [alert](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/alert_3) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/əˈlɜːt/` · **US:** `/əˈlɜːrt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ale/alert/alert__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ale/alert/alert__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सतर्क, जागरूक, चैतन्य
- **Examples:**
  - Suddenly he found himself awake and fully alert.
  - Two alert scientists spotted the mistake.
  - Try to stay alert while driving at night.
  - an alert mind

<a id="w-1018"></a>
### 1018. [alive](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/alive) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/əˈlaɪv/` · **US:** `/əˈlaɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ali/alive/alive__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ali/alive/alive__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सतर्क, भरा हुआ, जीवित
- **Examples:**
  - Is your mother still alive?
  - Doctors kept the baby alive for six weeks.
  - She had to steal food just to stay alive.
  - He was buried alive in the earthquake.

<a id="w-1019"></a>
### 1019. [allocate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/allocate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈæləkeɪt/` · **US:** `/ˈæləkeɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/all/alloc/allocate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/all/alloc/allocate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निर्धारित करना, नियत  करना, नियत करना
- **Examples:**
  - allocate something (for something) A large sum has been allocated for buying new books for the library.
  - allocate something (to somebody/something) They intend to allocate more places to mature students this year.
  - More resources are being allocated to the project.
  - allocate somebody/something sth The project is being allocated more resources.

<a id="w-1020"></a>
### 1020. [alter](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/alter) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɔːltə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈɔːltər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/alt/alter/alter__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/alt/alter/alter__us_4.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बदल देना, बदल जाना
- **Examples:**
  - Prices did not alter significantly during 2019.
  - He had altered so much I scarcely recognized him.
  - alter somebody/something His actions that day altered my perception of him.
  - The landscape has been radically altered by changes in the climate.

<a id="w-1021"></a>
### 1021. [alternative](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/alternative_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɔːlˈtɜːnətɪv/` · **US:** `/ɔːlˈtɜːrnətɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/alt/alter/alternative__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/alt/alter/alternative__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** एवजी, विकल्प, वैकल्पिक
- **Examples:**
  - The road was closed so we had to find an alternative route.
  - Can you offer an alternative explanation?
  - an alternative method of working
  - Do you have an alternative solution?

<a id="w-1022"></a>
### 1022. [amazing](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/amazing) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/əˈmeɪzɪŋ/` · **US:** `/əˈmeɪzɪŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ama/amazi/amazing__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ama/amazi/amazing__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** चमत्कार, आश्चर्यजनक, विस्मयकारी
- **Examples:**
  - an amazing feat/story/experience
  - That's amazing, isn't it?
  - The amazing thing is, he really believes he'll get away with it.
  - amazing to do something It was truly amazing to see how much work goes into a Broadway musical.

<a id="w-1023"></a>
### 1023. [analyze](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/analyse) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈænəlaɪz/` · **US:** `/ˈænəlaɪz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ana/analy/analyse__gb_6.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ana/analy/analyse__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मनोविश्लेषण  करना, मनोविश्लेषण करना
- **Examples:**
  - analyse something The job involves collecting and analysing data.
  - Researchers have analysed the results in detail using specialist software.
  - Learn to step back and critically analyse situations.
  - He tried to analyse his feelings.

<a id="w-1024"></a>
### 1024. [ancient](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/ancient) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈeɪnʃənt/` · **US:** `/ˈeɪnʃənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/anc/ancie/ancient__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/anc/ancie/ancient__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पुराना, प्राचीन, बहुत पुराना
- **Examples:**
  - Archaeologists are excavating the ruined temples of this ancient civilization.
  - ancient Greece/Egypt/Rome
  - the ancient city of Petra

<a id="w-1025"></a>
### 1025. [anger](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/anger_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈæŋɡə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈæŋɡər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ang/anger/anger__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ang/anger/anger__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** गुस्सा, क्रोध, रोष
- **Examples:**
  - She had to find a way to express her pent-up anger.
  - The plan provoked anger among local residents.
  - the growing anger and frustration of young unemployed people
  - in anger Jan slammed her fist on the desk in anger.

<a id="w-1026"></a>
### 1026. [angle](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/angle_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈæŋɡl/` · **US:** `/ˈæŋɡl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ang/angle/angle__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ang/angle/angle__us_1.mp3)

  - a 45° angle
  - the rocket’s angle of descent

<a id="w-1027"></a>
### 1027. [angry](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/angry) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈæŋɡri/` · **US:** `/ˈæŋɡri/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ang/angry/angry__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ang/angry/angry__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** गुस्सा, कटु, क्रोधित
- **Examples:**
  - Her behaviour really made me angry.
  - I started to get really angry and upset.
  - The players were attacked by an angry mob.
  - The comments provoked an angry response from union leaders.

<a id="w-1028"></a>
### 1028. [announce](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/announce) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈnaʊns/` · **US:** `/əˈnaʊns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ann/annou/announce__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ann/annou/announce__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** घोषणा, उद्घोषणा करना, घोषित  करना
- **Examples:**
  - announce something He officially announced his intention to resign at today's press conference.
  - They haven't formally announced their engagement yet.
  - (figurative) A ring at the doorbell announced Jack's arrival.
  - announce that… We are pleased to announce that all five candidates were successful.

<a id="w-1029"></a>
### 1029. [annual](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/annual_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈænjuəl/` · **US:** `/ˈænjuəl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ann/annua/annual__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ann/annua/annual__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वार्षिक
- **Examples:**
  - an annual meeting/event/report/conference
  - The 12th annual Music Festival runs from Friday 25th to Monday 28th October.
  - Funding will be awarded on an annual basis.
  - She organized this year's annual Christmas dinner dance.

<a id="w-1030"></a>
### 1030. [anticipate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/anticipate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ænˈtɪsɪpeɪt/` · **US:** `/ænˈtɪsɪpeɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ant/antic/anticipate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ant/antic/anticipate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** करना, सोच  रखना, उम्मीद करना
- **Examples:**
  - anticipate something We don't anticipate any major problems.
  - Our anticipated arrival time is 8.30.
  - The eagerly anticipated movie will be released next month.
  - anticipate doing something They anticipate moving to bigger premises by the end of the year.

<a id="w-1031"></a>
### 1031. [anxiety](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/anxiety) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/æŋˈzaɪəti/` · **US:** `/æŋˈzaɪəti/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/anx/anxie/anxiety__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/anx/anxie/anxiety__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उत्सुकता, चिंता, चिन्ता/व्याकुलता
- **Examples:**
  - acute/intense/deep anxiety
  - Some hospital patients experience high levels of anxiety.
  - Waiting for exam results is a time of great anxiety.

<a id="w-1032"></a>
### 1032. [apartment](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/apartment) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/əˈpɑːtmənt/` · **US:** `/əˈpɑːrtmənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/apa/apart/apartment__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/apa/apart/apartment__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कमरा, फ़्लैट, कमरे
- **Examples:**
  - an apartment building
  - I was renting a two-bedroom apartment in an old building.
  - A group of developers wants to build a high-rise apartment complex.
  - I lived on the ground floor of an apartment block.

<a id="w-1033"></a>
### 1033. [apologize](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/apologize) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈpɒlədʒaɪz/` · **US:** `/əˈpɑːlədʒaɪz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/apo/apolo/apologize__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/apo/apolo/apologize__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** क्षमा मांगना
- **Examples:**
  - Why should I apologize?
  - apologize to somebody Go and apologize to her.
  - He wanted to publicly apologize to his victims.
  - I want to apologize publicly on his behalf to those who have suffered.

<a id="w-1034"></a>
### 1034. [apparent](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/apparent) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/əˈpærənt/` · **US:** `/əˈpærənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/app/appar/apparent__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/app/appar/apparent__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रकट, स्पष्ट, साफ
- **Examples:**
  - Their devotion was apparent.
  - Then, for no apparent reason, the train suddenly stopped.
  - apparent from something that… It was apparent from her face that she was really upset.
  - apparent to somebody that… It soon became apparent to everyone that he couldn't sing.

<a id="w-1035"></a>
### 1035. [appeal](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/appeal_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/əˈpiːl/` · **US:** `/əˈpiːl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/app/appea/appeal__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/app/appea/appeal__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आकर्षण, पुनरावेदन, अपील
- **Examples:**
  - appeal for something to launch a TV appeal for donations to the charity
  - appeal to somebody He made an emotional appeal to voters.
  - appeal to somebody for something Police are making a fresh appeal to the public for any information that may help them.
  - appeal to somebody to do something The police made an appeal to the public to remain calm.

<a id="w-1036"></a>
### 1036. [applaud](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/applaud) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈplɔːd/` · **US:** `/əˈplɔːd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/app/appla/applaud__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/app/appla/applaud__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ताली बजा कर प्रशंसा करना, सराहना, ताली बजाना
- **Examples:**
  - He started to applaud and the others joined in.
  - applaud somebody They rose to applaud the speaker.
  - She was applauded as she came on stage.
  - The audience applauded loudly.

<a id="w-1037"></a>
### 1037. [applicant](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/applicant) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈæplɪkənt/` · **US:** `/ˈæplɪkənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/app/appli/applicant__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/app/appli/applicant__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रयोग, प्रार्थी/आवेदक, आवेदक
- **Examples:**
  - There were over 500 applicants for the job.
  - Successful applicants will receive notification within the week.

<a id="w-1038"></a>
### 1038. [appoint](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/appoint) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈpɔɪnt/` · **US:** `/əˈpɔɪnt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/app/appoi/appoint__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/app/appoi/appoint__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बनाना, नियुक्त करना, निर्धारित करना
- **Examples:**
  - appoint somebody They have appointed a new head teacher at my son's school.
  - appoint somebody to something She has recently been appointed to the committee.
  - appoint somebody (as) something They appointed him (as) captain of the English team.
  - She's been appointed British ambassador to the UN.

<a id="w-1039"></a>
### 1039. [appointment](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/appointment) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/əˈpɔɪntmənt/` · **US:** `/əˈpɔɪntmənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/app/appoi/appointment__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/app/appoi/appointment__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वचन, नियुक्ति, नौकरी
- **Examples:**
  - I've got a dental appointment at 3 o'clock.
  - to book/make/keep an appointment
  - Do you have an appointment?
  - She has a doctor's appointment in twenty minutes.

<a id="w-1040"></a>
### 1040. [appreciate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/appreciate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈpriːʃieɪt/` · **US:** `/əˈpriːʃieɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/app/appre/appreciate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/app/appre/appreciate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** क़दर करना, प्रशंसा करना, महसूस करना
- **Examples:**
  - Over the years he came to appreciate the beauty and tranquillity of the river.
  - His talents are not fully appreciated in that company.
  - Her family doesn't appreciate her.
  - You can't really appreciate foreign literature in translation.

<a id="w-1041"></a>
### 1041. [appetite](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/appetite) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈæpɪtaɪt/` · **US:** `/ˈæpɪtaɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/app/appet/appetite__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/app/appet/appetite__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** इच्छा, रुचि, भूख
- **Examples:**
  - He suffered from headaches and loss of appetite.
  - The walk gave me a good appetite.
  - Don't spoil your appetite by eating between meals.

<a id="w-1042"></a>
### 1042. [appropriate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/appropriate_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/əˈprəʊpriət/` · **US:** `/əˈprəʊpriət/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/app/appro/appropriate__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/app/appro/appropriate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उचित, उपयुक्त, यथोचित
- **Examples:**
  - an appropriate response/measure/method
  - Now that the problem has been identified, appropriate action can be taken.
  - Is now an appropriate time to make a speech?
  - Please debit my Mastercard/Visa/American Express card (delete as appropriate) (= cross out the options that do not apply).

<a id="w-1043"></a>
### 1043. [approve](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/approve) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈpruːv/` · **US:** `/əˈpruːv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/app/appro/approve__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/app/appro/approve__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्वीकार करना, पसन्द करना, पसंद करना
- **Examples:**
  - I told my mother I wanted to leave school but she didn't approve.
  - approve of somebody/something Do you approve of my idea?
  - She didn't quite approve of the way he was running things.
  - approve of somebody doing something She doesn't approve of me leaving school this year.

<a id="w-1044"></a>
### 1044. [approximate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/approximate)
- [Oxford Learner's Dictionary Search](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/approximate)
- **Meaning:** लगभग, समीप आना, लगभग सही अनुमान लगाना

<a id="w-1045"></a>
### 1045. [architect](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/architect_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɑːkɪtekt/` · **US:** `/ˈɑːrkɪtekt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/arc/archi/architect__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/arc/archi/architect__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** शिल्पकार, वास्तुकार, निर्माता
- **Examples:**
  - The house was designed by architect Louis Kahn.
  - She's a London-based architect and interior designer.
  - In 1721 he was named chief architect of the cathedral.

<a id="w-1046"></a>
### 1046. [architecture](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/architecture) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɑːkɪtektʃə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈɑːrkɪtektʃər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/arc/archi/architecture__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/arc/archi/architecture__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** शिल्प, वास्तुकला, शिल्प विद्या
- **Examples:**
  - to study architecture
  - She's a professor of architecture at the University of Oregon.
  - an architecture student/critic

<a id="w-1047"></a>
### 1047. [argument](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/argument) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɑːɡjumənt/` · **US:** `/ˈɑːrɡjumənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/arg/argum/argument__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/arg/argum/argument__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** तर्क, झगड़ा, विषय
- **Examples:**
  - to win/lose an argument
  - After some heated argument a decision was finally taken.
  - argument with somebody She got into an argument with the teacher.
  - argument about/over something He was killed during a violent argument over money.

<a id="w-1048"></a>
### 1048. [arise](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/arise) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈraɪz/` · **US:** `/əˈraɪz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ari/arise/arise__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ari/arise/arise__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उदय होना, उत्पन्न होना, निकलना
- **Examples:**
  - An opportunity arose to work in the United States.
  - Questions naturally arose as to who was responsible.
  - A serious problem can arise if the heart stops pumping effectively.
  - Children should be disciplined when the need arises (= when it is necessary).

<a id="w-1049"></a>
### 1049. [army](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/army) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɑːmi/` · **US:** `/ˈɑːrmi/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/arm/army_/army__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/arm/army_/army__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समूह, दल, भीड़
- **Examples:**
  - The two opposing armies faced each other across the battlefield.
  - He commanded the army of occupation after the war.

<a id="w-1050"></a>
### 1050. [arrangement](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/arrangement) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/əˈreɪndʒmənt/` · **US:** `/əˈreɪndʒmənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/arr/arran/arrangement__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/arr/arran/arrangement__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सजावट, प्रक्रिया, व्यवस्थापन
- **Examples:**
  - Travel and accommodation arrangements have been finalized.
  - arrangement for something I'll make arrangements for you to be met at the airport.

<a id="w-1051"></a>
### 1051. [arrest](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/arrest_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/əˈrest/` · **US:** `/əˈrest/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/arr/arres/arrest__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/arr/arres/arrest__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अवरोध
- **Examples:**
  - The police made several arrests.
  - under arrest She was under arrest on suspicion of murder.
  - Opposition leaders were put under house arrest (= not allowed to leave their houses).
  - He was placed under arrest by the New York City police.

<a id="w-1052"></a>
### 1052. [arrival](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/arrival) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/əˈraɪvl/` · **US:** `/əˈraɪvl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/arr/arriv/arrival__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/arr/arriv/arrival__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आने वाला, आगमन, जन्म
- **Examples:**
  - We apologize for the late arrival of the train.
  - the arrival of the mail in the morning
  - Our estimated time of arrival is 7.15.
  - I need to check our arrival time.

<a id="w-1053"></a>
### 1053. [arrow](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/arrow) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈærəʊ/` · **US:** `/ˈærəʊ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/arr/arrow/arrow__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/arr/arrow/arrow__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** तीर, शर, `  > चिह्न
- **Examples:**
  - a bow and arrow
  - to fire/shoot an arrow
  - The road continues as straight as an arrow.

<a id="w-1054"></a>
### 1054. [aspect](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/aspect) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈæspekt/` · **US:** `/ˈæspekt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/asp/aspec/aspect__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/asp/aspec/aspect__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दृष्टिकोण, गुण, रूप
- **Examples:**
  - aspect of something The book aims to cover all aspects of city life.
  - The exhibition will focus on various aspects of life and culture in the Middle East.
  - the most important aspect of the debate
  - She felt she had looked at the problem from every aspect.

<a id="w-1055"></a>
### 1055. [assess](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/assess) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈses/` · **US:** `/əˈses/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ass/asses/assess__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ass/asses/assess__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मूल्यांकन करना, निर्धारित करना, आँका जाना
- **Examples:**
  - assess somebody/something Accurately assessing environmental impacts is very complex.
  - to assess a patient’s needs
  - It's difficult to assess the effects of these changes.
  - Interviews allow you to assess the suitability of candidates.

<a id="w-1056"></a>
### 1056. [assessment](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/assessment) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/əˈsesmənt/` · **US:** `/əˈsesmənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ass/asses/assessment__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ass/asses/assessment__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निर्धारण, राय, आँकलन/मूल्यांकन
- **Examples:**
  - a detailed assessment of the risks involved
  - An adequate environmental impact assessment was not carried out on the bypass project.
  - We conducted the initial assessments defining the scope of work.
  - The city hired them to do a structural assessment of the damage.

<a id="w-1057"></a>
### 1057. [asset](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/asset) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈæset/` · **US:** `/ˈæset/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ass/asset/asset__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ass/asset/asset__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** गुण, सम्पत्ति, खूबी।
- **Examples:**
  - In his job, patience is an invaluable asset.
  - Being able to speak a foreign language is a major asset.
  - I’m not sure if his forcefulness is an asset or a liability.
  - asset to somebody/something She'll be an asset to the team.

<a id="w-1058"></a>
### 1058. [assign](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/assign) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈsaɪn/` · **US:** `/əˈsaɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ass/assig/assign__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ass/assig/assign__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निश्चित करना, देना, नियुक्त करना
- **Examples:**
  - assign something (to somebody) The teacher assigned a different task to each of the children.
  - The two large classrooms have been assigned to us.
  - assign somebody something We have been assigned the two large classrooms.
  - The teacher assigned each of the children a different task.

<a id="w-1059"></a>
### 1059. [assist](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/assist_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈsɪst/` · **US:** `/əˈsɪst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ass/assis/assist__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ass/assis/assist__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मदद करना, सहायता देना, सुविधा देना
- **Examples:**
  - Anyone willing to assist can contact this number.
  - assist in/with something He assisted in the development of the business strategy.
  - We are looking for people who would be willing to assist in the group's work.
  - assist somebody We'll do all we can to assist you.

<a id="w-1060"></a>
### 1060. [assistant](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/assistant_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/əˈsɪstənt/` · **US:** `/əˈsɪstənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ass/assis/assistant__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ass/assis/assistant__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सहायक
- **Examples:**
  - the assistant manager/director/commissioner
  - She is an assistant professor of psychology at Ohio State University.
  - Assistant Chief Constable Owen
  - Assistant Attorney General William Weld

<a id="w-1061"></a>
### 1061. [associate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/associate_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈsəʊʃieɪt/` · **US:** `/əˈsəʊʃieɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ass/assoc/associate__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ass/assoc/associate__us_2_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मिलना, मिलाना, सम्बंद्ध होना
- **Examples:**
  - associate somebody/something with somebody/something I always associate the smell of baking with my childhood.
  - He is closely associated in the public mind with horror movies.
  - Most people immediately associate addictions with drugs, alcohol and cigarettes.
  - associate somebody/something You wouldn’t normally associate these two writers—their styles are completely different.

<a id="w-1062"></a>
### 1062. [association](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/association) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/əˌsəʊʃiˈeɪʃn/` · **US:** `/əˌsəʊʃiˈeɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ass/assoc/association__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ass/assoc/association__us_2_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** संबंध, समिति, संस्था
- **Examples:**
  - Do you belong to any professional or trade associations?
  - a residents’ association

<a id="w-1063"></a>
### 1063. [assume](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/assume) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈsjuːm/` · **US:** `/əˈsuːm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ass/assum/assume__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ass/assum/assume__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समझना, रूप धारण करना, मानना
- **Examples:**
  - assume (that)… It is reasonable to assume (that) the economy will continue to improve.
  - It's probably safe to assume (that) we'll be hearing more about this story.
  - Let us assume for a moment that the plan succeeds.
  - She would, he assumed, be home at the usual time.

<a id="w-1064"></a>
### 1064. [assumption](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/assumption) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/əˈsʌmpʃn/` · **US:** `/əˈsʌmpʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ass/assum/assumption__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ass/assum/assumption__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मान्यता, पुर्वानुमान, ग्रहण
- **Examples:**
  - an underlying/implicit assumption
  - We need to challenge some of the basic assumptions of Western philosophy.
  - We are working on the assumption that everyone invited will turn up.
  - It was impossible to make assumptions about people's reactions.

<a id="w-1065"></a>
### 1065. [assure](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/assure) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈʃɔː(r)/` · **US:** `/əˈʃʊr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ass/assur/assure__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ass/assur/assure__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निश्चित करना, आश्वासन देना, निश्चित  करना
- **Examples:**
  - assure somebody (that)… You think I did it deliberately, but I assure you (that) I did not.
  - Let me assure you that I will try to help you.
  - We were assured that everything possible was being done.
  - She's perfectly safe, I can assure you.

<a id="w-1066"></a>
### 1066. [athlete](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/athlete) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈæθliːt/` · **US:** `/ˈæθliːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ath/athle/athlete__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ath/athle/athlete__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** व्यायामी
- **Examples:**
  - Olympic athletes
  - Depression is thought to be common among elite athletes.
  - a training facility for amateur and professional athletes
  - Her company is designing part of the Olympic athletes' village.

<a id="w-1067"></a>
### 1067. [atmosphere](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/atmosphere) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈætməsfɪə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈætməsfɪr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/atm/atmos/atmosphere__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/atm/atmos/atmosphere__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वायुमण्डल, परिमण्डल, वातावरण
- **Examples:**
  - Wind power doesn't release carbon dioxide into the atmosphere.
  - greenhouse gases in the earth's atmosphere
  - Volcanoes and hot springs can emit methane into the atmosphere.
  - the upper atmosphere

<a id="w-1068"></a>
### 1068. [attach](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/attach) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈtætʃ/` · **US:** `/əˈtætʃ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/att/attac/attach__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/att/attac/attach__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लगाना, देना, संलग्न  करना
- **Examples:**
  - attach something I attach a copy of my notes for your information.
  - A copy of his document is attached as Appendix 3 at the end of this book.
  - attach something to something Attach the coupon to the front of your letter.
  - (figurative) They have attached a number of conditions to the agreement (= said that the conditions must be part of the agreement)

<a id="w-1069"></a>
### 1069. [attempt](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/attempt_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/əˈtempt/` · **US:** `/əˈtempt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/att/attem/attempt__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/att/attem/attempt__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रयास, प्रयत्न
- **Examples:**
  - I passed my driving test at the first attempt.
  - attempt to do something They made no attempt to escape.
  - The launch will go ahead despite the failure of previous attempts to create an online business.
  - Why have repeated attempts to find a permanent solution to the problem failed ?

<a id="w-1070"></a>
### 1070. [attend](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/attend) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈtend/` · **US:** `/əˈtend/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/att/atten/attend__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/att/atten/attend__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ध्यान केंद्रित करना, साथ जाना, पर ध्यान देना
- **Examples:**
  - We'd like as many people as possible to attend.
  - attend something The meeting was attended by 90% of shareholders.
  - to attend a wedding/funeral/service/ceremony
  - Everyone is welcome to attend the free event.

<a id="w-1071"></a>
### 1071. [attitude](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/attitude) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈætɪtjuːd/` · **US:** `/ˈætɪtuːd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/att/attit/attitude__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/att/attit/attitude__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मुद्रा, रवैया, प्रवृति
- **Examples:**
  - attitude towards somebody/something These societies have to change their attitudes towards women.
  - the government’s attitude towards single parents
  - to have a positive/negative attitude towards somebody/something
  - attitude to somebody/something changes in public attitudes to marriage

<a id="w-1072"></a>
### 1072. [attract](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/attract) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈtrækt/` · **US:** `/əˈtrækt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/att/attra/attract__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/att/attra/attract__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आकर्षित करना, आकर्षित  करना, आकृष्ट करना
- **Examples:**
  - be attracted by somebody/something I had always been attracted by the idea of working abroad.
  - attract somebody (to somebody/something) What first attracted me to her was her sense of humour.
  - She finds herself increasingly attracted to them and their lifestyle.

<a id="w-1073"></a>
### 1073. [attraction](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/attraction) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/əˈtrækʃn/` · **US:** `/əˈtrækʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/att/attra/attraction__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/att/attra/attraction__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आकर्षण
- **Examples:**
  - Buckingham Palace is a major tourist attraction.
  - The main attraction at Giverny is Monet's garden.
  - Attractions at the fair include a mini-circus, clowns, dancers and a jazz band.
  - Feeding the animals proved a popular attraction for visitors to the farm.

<a id="w-1074"></a>
### 1074. [attribute](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/attribute_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈætrɪbjuːt/` · **US:** `/ˈætrɪbjuːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/att/attri/attribute__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/att/attri/attribute__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विशेषता, लक्ष्ण, सहजगुण
- **Examples:**
  - Patience is one of the most important attributes in a teacher.
  - The most basic attribute of all animals is consciousness.

<a id="w-1075"></a>
### 1075. [audience](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/audience) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɔːdiəns/` · **US:** `/ˈɔːdiəns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/aud/audie/audience__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/aud/audie/audience__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** श्रोतागण, श्रोता, औपचारिक बैठक
- **Examples:**
  - The audience was/were clapping for 10 minutes.
  - The museum is trying to attract a wider audience.
  - The debate was televised in front of a live audience.
  - an audience of 10 000

### 1076. [authentic](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/authentic) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɔːˈθentɪk/` · **US:** `/ɔːˈθentɪk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/aut/authe/authentic__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/aut/authe/authentic__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-1078"></a>
### 1078. [average](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/average_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈævərɪdʒ/` · **US:** `/ˈævərɪdʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ave/avera/average__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ave/avera/average__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सामान्य, औसत  होना, औसत
- **Examples:**
  - The average age of participants was 52 years.
  - an average rate/cost/price
  - an average annual income/wage/salary
  - Average daily summer temperatures are about 20°C .

<a id="w-1079"></a>
### 1079. [award](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/award_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/əˈwɔːd/` · **US:** `/əˈwɔːrd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/awa/award/award__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/awa/award/award__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अनुदान, पुरस्कार, दंड
- **Examples:**
  - He was nominated for the best actor award.
  - the UK's most prestigious literary award
  - the Housing Design Award
  - The mayor will present the awards.

<a id="w-1080"></a>
### 1080. [aware](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/aware) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/əˈweə(r)/` · **US:** `/əˈwer/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/awa/aware/aware__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/awa/aware/aware__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अवगत, परिचित, सचेत
- **Examples:**
  - As you're aware, this is not a new problem.
  - As far as I'm aware, nobody has done anything about it.
  - acutely/painfully/keenly (= very) aware
  - aware of something He was well aware of the problem.

<a id="w-1081"></a>
### 1081. [awareness](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/awareness) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/əˈweənəs/` · **US:** `/əˈwernəs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/awa/aware/awareness__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/awa/aware/awareness__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जानकारी, जागरूकता, जानकारी/अभिज्ञता
- **Examples:**
  - awareness of something an awareness of the importance of eating a healthy diet
  - There was an almost complete lack of awareness of the issues involved.
  - to raise/heighten/increase public awareness of something
  - a greater/a growing/an increasing awareness of something

<a id="w-1082"></a>
### 1082. [awkward](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/awkward) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɔːkwəd/` · **US:** `/ˈɔːkwərd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/awk/awkwa/awkward__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/awk/awkwa/awkward__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अनुपयुक्त, भद्दा, तकलीफ़देह
- **Examples:**
  - There was an awkward silence.
  - I felt awkward because they obviously wanted to be alone.

<a id="w-1083"></a>
### 1083. [background](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/background) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈbækɡraʊnd/` · **US:** `/ˈbækɡraʊnd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bac/backg/background__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bac/backg/background__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वर्ग, आधार, ज्ञान
- **Examples:**
  - somebody's ethnic/genetic/cultural background
  - somebody's family/educational background
  - The job would suit someone with a business background.
  - We come from very different backgrounds but we get on well.

<a id="w-1084"></a>
### 1084. [ban](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/ban_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/bæn/` · **US:** `/bæn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/ban/ban__/ban__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/ban/ban__/ban__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निषेध
- **Examples:**
  - a smoking ban
  - A majority of people are against the ban.
  - ban on something They are calling for a blanket ban (= complete ban) on the use of phones while driving.
  - They have imposed a total ban on the import of seal skins.

<a id="w-1085"></a>
### 1085. [barrier](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/barrier) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈbæriə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈbæriər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bar/barri/barrier__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bar/barri/barrier__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अवरौध, बाधा, अवरोध
- **Examples:**
  - The crowd had to stand behind barriers.
  - Concrete barriers were erected around the site.
  - The crowd managed to break through the barriers and get onto the pitch.

<a id="w-1086"></a>
### 1086. [basis](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/basis) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈbeɪsɪs/` · **US:** `/ˈbeɪsɪs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bas/basis/basis__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bas/basis/basis__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आधार
- **Examples:**
  - We are in contact on a regular basis.
  - to be employed on a permanent/temporary/part-time basis
  - Fatal accidents occur on our roads on a daily basis.
  - on a day-to-day/weekly/monthly basis

<a id="w-1087"></a>
### 1087. [battle](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/battle_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈbætl/` · **US:** `/ˈbætl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bat/battl/battle__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bat/battl/battle__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लड़ाई/युध्द, युध्द, संगर्ष
- **Examples:**
  - Napoleon was defeated at the Battle of Waterloo.
  - in battle His father had been killed in battle.
  - to go into battle
  - battle with somebody Hundreds of protesters fought running battles with the police.

<a id="w-1088"></a>
### 1088. [bear](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/bear_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/beə(r)/` · **US:** `/ber/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bea/bear_/bear__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bea/bear_/bear__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** रूखा व्याक्ति, मंदा, भालू का बच्चा
- **Examples:**
  - a black bear
  - A little bear cub ran right across the road in front of us.

<a id="w-1089"></a>
### 1089. [beauty](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/beauty) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈbjuːti/` · **US:** `/ˈbjuːti/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bea/beaut/beauty__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bea/beaut/beauty__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सुन्दरता, अच्छी चीज़, सौंदर्य
- **Examples:**
  - beauty of something the beauty of the sunset/of poetry/of his singing
  - The woods were designated an area of outstanding natural beauty.
  - beauty products/treatments (= intended to make a person more beautiful)
  - She was a contestant in the Miss World beauty pageant.

<a id="w-1090"></a>
### 1090. [behave](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/behave) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/bɪˈheɪv/` · **US:** `/bɪˈheɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/beh/behav/behave__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/beh/behav/behave__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** व्यवहार करना, चलना, बर्ताव करना
- **Examples:**
  - The doctor behaved very unprofessionally.
  - They behaved very badly towards their guests.
  - He behaved like a true gentleman.
  - They behave differently when you're not around.

<a id="w-1091"></a>
### 1091. [belief](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/belief) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/bɪˈliːf/` · **US:** `/bɪˈliːf/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bel/belie/belief__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bel/belie/belief__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भरोसा, दृढ विश्वास, दृढ  विश्वास
- **Examples:**
  - belief in something/somebody I admire his passionate belief in what he is doing.
  - belief in God/democracy
  - The incident has shaken my belief (= made me have less confidence) in the police.
  - belief that… They share a belief that there is life after death.

<a id="w-1092"></a>
### 1092. [belong](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/belong) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/bɪˈlɒŋ/` · **US:** `/bɪˈlɔːŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bel/belon/belong__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bel/belon/belong__us_6.mp3)
- **Meaning:** होना, रखना, सम्बन्ध रखना
- **Examples:**
  - Where do these plates belong (= where are they kept)?
  - Are you sure these documents belong together?
  - Several of the points you raise do not belong in this discussion.
  - A person like that does not belong in teaching.

<a id="w-1093"></a>
### 1093. [beneath](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/beneath_1) *(preposition)*
- **UK:** `/bɪˈniːθ/` · **US:** `/bɪˈniːθ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/ben/benea/beneath__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/ben/benea/beneath__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** नीचे
- **Examples:**
  - They found the body buried beneath a pile of leaves.
  - The boat sank beneath the waves.

<a id="w-1094"></a>
### 1094. [beside](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/beside) *(preposition)*
- **UK:** `/bɪˈsaɪd/` · **US:** `/bɪˈsaɪd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bes/besid/beside__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bes/besid/beside__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पास
- **Examples:**
  - He sat beside her all night.
  - a mill beside a stream

<a id="w-1095"></a>
### 1095. [bias](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/bias_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈbaɪəs/` · **US:** `/ˈbaɪəs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bia/bias_/bias__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bia/bias_/bias__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पक्षपात, झुकाव, तिरछा
- **Examples:**
  - accusations of political bias in news programmes (= that reports are unfair and show favour to one political party)
  - Employers must consider all candidates impartially and without bias.
  - Some institutions still have a strong bias against women.
  - The article examines gender bias in our schools.

<a id="w-1096"></a>
### 1096. [bind](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/bind_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/baɪnd/` · **US:** `/baɪnd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bin/bind_/bind__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bin/bind_/bind__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लगाना, भरना, जकड़ना
- **Examples:**
  - bind somebody/something to something She was bound to a chair.
  - bind somebody/something together They bound his hands together.
  - bind somebody/something He was left bound and gagged (= tied up and with a piece of cloth tied over his mouth).

<a id="w-1097"></a>
### 1097. [blame](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/blame_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/bleɪm/` · **US:** `/bleɪm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bla/blame/blame__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bla/blame/blame__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आरोप, दोष, उत्तरदायित्व
- **Examples:**
  - Why do I always get the blame for everything that goes wrong?
  - to lay/put the blame for something on somebody
  - The government will have to take the blame for the riots.
  - The real estate industry deserves a fair share of the blame for this failure.

<a id="w-1098"></a>
### 1098. [blind](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/blind_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/blaɪnd/` · **US:** `/blaɪnd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bli/blind/blind__gb_11.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bli/blind/blind__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अविवेचित, असफल, बन्द
- **Examples:**
  - Doctors think he will go blind.
  - She went blind at the age of ten.
  - blind and partially sighted people
  - One of her parents is blind.

<a id="w-1099"></a>
### 1099. [block](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/block_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/blɒk/` · **US:** `/blɑːk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/blo/block/block__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/blo/block/block__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कील, बाधा, अवरोध
- **Examples:**
  - The houses are made of concrete blocks with tin roofs.
  - block of something a block of ice/wood
  - The wall was made from massive blocks of stone.

<a id="w-1100"></a>
### 1100. [boast](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/boast_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/bəʊst/` · **US:** `/bəʊst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/boa/boast/boast__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/boa/boast/boast__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** डींग मारना, गौरव, इतराना
- **Examples:**
  - I don't want to boast, but I can actually speak six languages.
  - boast about something She is always boasting about how wonderful her children are.
  - boast of something He openly boasted of his skill as a burglar.
  - boast that… Sam boasted that she could beat anyone at poker.

## Words 1,101 – 1,200

<a id="w-1101"></a>
### 1101. [bond](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/bond_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/bɒnd/` · **US:** `/bɑːnd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bon/bond_/bond__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bon/bond_/bond__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बंधन, बन्धन, ऋण पट्र
- **Examples:**
  - the bond that links us
  - bond between A and B A bond of friendship had been forged between them.
  - The agreement strengthened the bonds between the two countries.
  - the special bond between mother and child

<a id="w-1102"></a>
### 1102. [border](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/border_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈbɔːdə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈbɔːrdər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bor/borde/border__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bor/borde/border__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** किनारा, सीमा, पट्टी
- **Examples:**
  - I live in a small town in the US, near the Canadian border.
  - Thousands of people cross the border every day.
  - They took steps to secure the border.
  - to seal/close the border

<a id="w-1103"></a>
### 1103. [borrow](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/borrow) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈbɒrəʊ/` · **US:** `/ˈbɔːrəʊ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bor/borro/borrow__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bor/borro/borrow__us_2_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लेना, कर्ज़ लेना, ऋण लेना
- **Examples:**
  - borrow something Can I borrow your umbrella?
  - borrow something from somebody/something Members can borrow up to ten books from the library at any one time.
  - borrow something off somebody (British English, informal) I borrowed the tools I needed off my brother.

<a id="w-1104"></a>
### 1104. [bother](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/bother_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈbɒðə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈbɑːðər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bot/bothe/bother__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bot/bothe/bother__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** परेशान करना, कष्ट देना, तंग करना
- **Examples:**
  - ‘Shall I wait?’ ‘No, don't bother’.
  - I don't know why I bother! Nobody ever listens!
  - If that’s all the thanks I get, I won’t bother in future!
  - bother with/about something It's not worth bothering with (= using) an umbrella—the car's just outside.

<a id="w-1105"></a>
### 1105. [bottle](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/bottle_1) *(noun)*

- **Meaning:** साहस, शराब, शीशी
- **Examples:**
  - a wine/beer/milk/water bottle
  - Put the top back on the bottle.
  - a plastic/glass bottle
  - To open the bottle, you twist and pull out the stopper.

<a id="w-1109"></a>

### 1110. [brand](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/brand_1) *(noun)*

- **UK:** `/breɪv/` · **US:** `/breɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bra/brave/brave__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bra/brave/brave__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-1112"></a>

- **UK:** `/breθ/` · **US:** `/breθ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bre/breat/breath__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bre/breat/breath__us_1.mp3)

  - (figurative) Their ambitions were finally dead and buried.

### 1134. [category](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/category) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkætəɡəri/` · **US:** `/ˈkætəɡɔːri/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cat/categ/category__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cat/categ/category__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वर्ग, वर्गीकृत ,वर्गीकरण
- **Examples:**
  - These are the nominees from each category.
  - in a category He competed in the youngest age category.
  - Students over 25 fall into a different category.
  - The results can be divided into three broad categories.

<a id="w-1135"></a>
### 1135. [celebrate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/celebrate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈselɪbreɪt/` · **US:** `/ˈselɪbreɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cel/celeb/celebrate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cel/celeb/celebrate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मनाना, पादरी का कार्य  करना, प्रचार करना
- **Examples:**
  - Jake's passed his exams. We're going out to celebrate.
  - We had good reason to celebrate.
  - celebrate something to celebrate a birthday
  - We celebrated our 25th wedding anniversary in Florence.

<a id="w-1136"></a>
### 1136. [ceremony](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/ceremony) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈserəməni/` · **US:** `/ˈserəməʊni/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cer/cerem/ceremony__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cer/cerem/ceremony__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अवसर, अतिशयोक्ति, विधि
- **Examples:**

  - an awards/opening ceremony
  - a wedding/marriage/graduation ceremony
  - a religious/civil ceremony

### 1137. [chain](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/chain_1) *(noun)*

- **Meaning:** बंधन, क्रम, छल्ला

### 1138. [chair](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/chair_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/tʃeə(r)/` · **US:** `/tʃer/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/chair/chair__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/chair/chair__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** गायक  दल का स्थानअ, कुर्सी, अध्यक्ष

  - in a chair an old man asleep in a chair (= an armchair)

### 1139. [challenge](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/challenge_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈtʃælɪndʒ/` · **US:** `/ˈtʃælɪndʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/chall/challenge__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/chall/challenge__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ललकार, दावा, आपत्ति
- **Examples:**
  - a tough/major/significant challenge
  - The role will be the biggest challenge of his acting career.
  - She still faces many challenges (= has to deal with them).
  - Destruction of the environment is one of the most serious challenges we face.

- **UK:** `/ˈtʃæmpiən/` · **US:** `/ˈtʃæmpiən/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/champ/champion__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/champ/champion__us_2.mp3)

  - the world/European/national/Olympic champion

  - the reigning champion (= the person who is champion now)
  - the world basketball champions

- **UK:** `/ˈtʃænl/` · **US:** `/ˈtʃænl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/chann/channel__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/chann/channel__us_1.mp3)

- **UK:** `/ˈtʃæptə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈtʃæptər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/chapt/chapter__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/chapt/chapter__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सभा, याजक सभा, अध्याय

<a id="w-1143"></a>
### 1143. [characteristic](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/characteristic_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˌkærəktəˈrɪstɪk/` · **US:** `/ˌkærəktəˈrɪstɪk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/chara/characteristic__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/chara/characteristic__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विशेष
- **Examples:**
  - characteristic of something/somebody Community support of families is characteristic of many societies.
  - This is just one example of the rudeness that was so characteristic of him.
  - the characteristic features of his style of painting
  - She spoke with characteristic enthusiasm.

<a id="w-1144"></a>
### 1144. [characterize](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/characterize) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkærəktəraɪz/` · **US:** `/ˈkærəktəraɪz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/chara/characterize__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/chara/characterize__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** की विशेषता होना, चरित्र चित्रण करना, विशेषता होना
- **Examples:**
  - the rolling hills that characterize this part of England
  - The city is characterized by tall modern buildings in steel and glass.

<a id="w-1145"></a>
### 1145. [charge](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/charge_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/tʃɑːdʒ/` · **US:** `/tʃɑːrdʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/charg/charge__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/charg/charge__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उत्तरदायी, दाम, पद

  - Delivery is free of charge.

### 1146. [charity](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/charity) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈtʃærəti/` · **US:** `/ˈtʃærəti/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/chari/charity__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/chari/charity__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उदारता, दया, दान

- **UK:** `/tʃɑːt/` · **US:** `/tʃɑːrt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/chart/chart__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/chart/chart__us_1.mp3)

  - The accompanying chart shows data for eight countries.
  - a sales chart (= showing the level of a company’s sales)

- **UK:** `/tʃeɪs/` · **US:** `/tʃeɪs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cha/chase/chase__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cha/chase/chase__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - The thieves were caught by police after a short chase.
  - a high-speed car chase
  - We lost him in the narrow streets and had to give up the chase (= stop chasing him).
  - to take up the chase (= start chasing somebody)

### 1149. [cheap](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/cheap_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/tʃiːp/` · **US:** `/tʃiːp/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/che/cheap/cheap__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/che/cheap/cheap__us_1.mp3)

- **UK:** `/tʃɪə(r)/` · **US:** `/tʃɪr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/che/cheer/cheer__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/che/cheer/cheer__us_1.mp3)

- **UK:** `/tʃiːf/` · **US:** `/tʃiːf/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/chi/chief/chief__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/chi/chief/chief__us_1.mp3)

- **UK:** `/ˈtʃaɪldhʊd/` · **US:** `/ˈtʃaɪldhʊd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/chi/child/childhood__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/chi/child/childhood__us_3.mp3)

  - She had a happy childhood.
