### 1514. [folk](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/folk_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/fəʊk/` · **US:** `/fəʊk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fol/folk_/folk__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fol/folk_/folk__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-1515"></a>
### 1515. [forecast](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/forecast_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈfɔːkɑːst/` · **US:** `/ˈfɔːrkæst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/for/forec/forecast__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/for/forec/forecast__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पूर्वानुमान
- **Examples:**
  - The sales forecasts are encouraging.
  - It is difficult to make long-term forecasts for a fast-growing industry.
  - The forecast said there would be sunny intervals and showers.
  - The shipping forecast gave warnings of gales.

<a id="w-1516"></a>
### 1516. [forgive](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/forgive) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/fəˈɡɪv/` · **US:** `/fərˈɡɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/for/forgi/forgive__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/for/forgi/forgive__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ऋण मुक्त कर  देना, क्षमा करना, माफ  करना
- **Examples:**
  - forgive somebody Can you ever forgive me?
  - forgive yourself I'd never forgive myself if she heard the truth from someone else.
  - forgive somebody/yourself for something I'll never forgive her for what she did.
  - forgive somebody/yourself for doing something She never forgave him for losing her ring.

<a id="w-1517"></a>
### 1517. [formal](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/formal) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈfɔːml/` · **US:** `/ˈfɔːrml/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/for/forma/formal__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/for/forma/formal__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** औपचारिक

### 1518. [format](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/format_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈfɔːmæt/` · **US:** `/ˈfɔːrmæt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/for/forma/format__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/for/forma/format__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-1519"></a>

- **UK:** `/fɔːθ/` · **US:** `/fɔːrθ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/for/forth/forth__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/for/forth/forth__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आगे

<a id="w-1520"></a>
### 1520. [foundation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/foundation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/faʊnˈdeɪʃn/` · **US:** `/faʊnˈdeɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fou/found/foundation__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fou/found/foundation__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आधार, प्रतिष्ठान, संस्था
- **Examples:**
  - Respect and friendship provide a solid foundation for marriage.
  - He laid the foundation of Japan's modern economy.
  - These stories have no foundation (= are not based on any facts).
  - without foundation The rumour is totally without foundation (= is not based on any facts).

### 1521. [frame](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/frame_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/freɪm/` · **US:** `/freɪm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fra/frame/frame__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fra/frame/frame__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-1522"></a>
### 1522. [framework](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/framework) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈfreɪmwɜːk/` · **US:** `/ˈfreɪmwɜːrk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fra/frame/framework__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fra/frame/framework__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-1523"></a>
### 1523. [freeze](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/freeze_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/friːz/` · **US:** `/friːz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fre/freez/freeze__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fre/freez/freeze__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जमना, ठंढा करके सुरक्षित रखना, स्थिर रखना
- **Examples:**
  - Water freezes at 0°C.
  - It's so cold that even the river has frozen.
  - freeze something The cold weather had frozen the ground.
  - freeze + adj. The clothes froze solid on the washing line.

<a id="w-1524"></a>
### 1524. [frequent](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/frequent_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈfriːkwənt/` · **US:** `/ˈfriːkwənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fre/frequ/frequent__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fre/frequ/frequent__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अक्सर
- **Examples:**
  - He is a frequent visitor to this country.
  - Her calls became less frequent.
  - There is a frequent bus service into the centre of town.
  - How frequent is this word (= how often does it occur in the language)?

<a id="w-1525"></a>
### 1525. [frequently](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/frequently) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈfriːkwəntli/` · **US:** `/ˈfriːkwəntli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fre/frequ/frequently__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fre/frequ/frequently__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बहुधा, प्रायः
- **Examples:**
  - Buses run frequently between the city and the airport.
  - some of the most frequently asked questions about the internet
  - It was a word he used frequently.
  - Her father, a pilot, was frequently absent from home.

### 1526. [fuel](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/fuel_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈfjuːəl/` · **US:** `/ˈfjuːəl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fue/fuel_/fuel__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fue/fuel_/fuel__us_1_rr.mp3)

  - The plant reprocesses spent fuel from nuclear power stations.

<a id="w-1527"></a>
### 1527. [function](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/function_1) *(noun)*

<a id="w-1532"></a>
### 1532. [gain](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/gain_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɡeɪn/` · **US:** `/ɡeɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gai/gain_/gain__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gai/gain_/gain__us_1.mp3)

### 1533. [gap](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/gap) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɡæp/` · **US:** `/ɡæp/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gap/gap__/gap__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gap/gap__/gap__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-1535"></a>
### 1535. [gather](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/gather) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɡæðə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈɡæðər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gat/gathe/gather__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gat/gathe/gather__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्राप्त करना, समझना, काटना
- **Examples:**
  - A crowd soon gathered.
  - + adv./prep. His supporters gathered in the main square.
  - Can you all gather round? I've got something to tell you.
  - The whole family gathered together at Ray's home.

<a id="w-1536"></a>
### 1536. [gaze](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/gaze_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɡeɪz/` · **US:** `/ɡeɪz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gaz/gaze_/gaze__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gaz/gaze_/gaze__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** एक्टक देखने  ताला, टकटकी, एक्टक देखने ताला
- **Examples:**
  - He met her gaze (= looked at her while she looked at him).
  - She dropped her gaze (= stopped looking).
  - In his last years, he lived almost entirely secluded from the public gaze (= away from public attention).

<a id="w-1537"></a>
### 1537. [gear](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/gear_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɡɪə(r)/` · **US:** `/ɡɪr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gea/gear_/gear__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gea/gear_/gear__us_1.mp3)

  - The car pulled up with a screech of brakes and a grinding of gears.

<a id="w-1538"></a>
### 1538. [gender](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/gender) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈdʒendə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈdʒendər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gen/gende/gender__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gen/gende/gender__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-1539"></a>
### 1539. [generate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/generate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈdʒenəreɪt/` · **US:** `/ˈdʒenəreɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gen/gener/generate__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gen/gener/generate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पैदा करना, पैदा  करना, कमाना
- **Examples:**

  - to generate heat/power

<a id="w-1540"></a>
### 1540. [generation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/generation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌdʒenəˈreɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌdʒenəˈreɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gen/gener/generation__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gen/gener/generation__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** युग, प्रजनन, युगअ
- **Examples:**
  - the younger/older generation
  - My generation have grown up with the internet.
  - I often wonder what future generations will make of our efforts.
  - The hopes and dreams of youth today are different from those of previous generations.

<a id="w-1541"></a>

- **UK:** `/dʒəˈnetɪk/` · **US:** `/dʒəˈnetɪk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gen/genet/genetic__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gen/genet/genetic__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - genetic and environmental factors
  - genetic abnormalities
  - There are about 4 000 inherited human genetic diseases.
  - genetic research/techniques

<a id="w-1542"></a>
### 1542. [genuine](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/genuine) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈdʒenjuɪn/` · **US:** `/ˈdʒenjuɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gen/genui/genuine__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gen/genui/genuine__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** असली, सच्चा, अमिश्रित/वास्तविक
- **Examples:**
  - Is the painting a genuine Picasso?
  - Fake designer watches are sold at a fraction of the price of the genuine article.
  - She is a genuine contender for the title.
  - The document is not considered genuine.

<a id="w-1543"></a>
### 1543. [gesture](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/gesture_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈdʒestʃə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈdʒestʃər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/ges/gestu/gesture__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/ges/gestu/gesture__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मुद्रा, भाव  भंगिमा, भाव
- **Examples:**
  - He made a rude gesture at the driver of the other car.
  - She finished what she had to say with a gesture of despair.
  - They communicated entirely by gesture.

<a id="w-1544"></a>
### 1544. [ghost](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/ghost_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɡəʊst/` · **US:** `/ɡəʊst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gho/ghost/ghost__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gho/ghost/ghost__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भूत, प्रेतात्मा, फीकी
- **Examples:**
  - Do you believe in ghosts (= believe that they exist)?
  - ghost of somebody The ghost of her father had come back to haunt her.
  - He looked as if he had seen a ghost (= looked very frightened)
  - The ghost hunters have so far found nothing.

### 1545. [giant](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/giant_1) *(adjective)*

### 1546. [gift](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/gift_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɡɪft/` · **US:** `/ɡɪft/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gif/gift_/gift__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gif/gift_/gift__us_1.mp3)

  - gift from somebody The watch was a gift from my mother.

### 1547. [glance](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/glance_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɡlɑːns/` · **US:** `/ɡlæns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gla/glanc/glance__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gla/glanc/glance__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**

  - a cursory/brief/casual/furtive glance

  - She shot him a sideways glance.

<a id="w-1548"></a>
### 1548. [glimpse](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/glimpse_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɡlɪmps/` · **US:** `/ɡlɪmps/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gli/glimp/glimpse__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gli/glimp/glimpse__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** झलक, झाँकी, ईक्षा
- **Examples:**
  - glimpse (of somebody/something) He caught a glimpse of her in the crowd.
  - I came up on deck to get my first glimpse of the island.
  - Fans crowded the street outside waiting for a glimpse of their hero.
  - glimpse at somebody/something I just got a glimpse at the baby, but she was very cute.

- **UK:** `/ˈɡlɔːri/` · **US:** `/ˈɡlɔːri/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/glo/glory/glory__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/glo/glory/glory__us_1.mp3)

- **UK:** `/ɡræb/` · **US:** `/ɡræb/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gra/grab_/grab__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gra/grab_/grab__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**

  - He grabbed hold of me and wouldn't let go.

  - Women have gradually become more involved in the decision-making process.

<a id="w-1557"></a>
### 1557. [grateful](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/grateful) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɡreɪtfl/` · **US:** `/ˈɡreɪtfl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gra/grate/grateful__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gra/grate/grateful__us_1.mp3)

  - grateful for something She said she was grateful for the support shown by the community.
  - grateful to somebody for something I am extremely grateful to all the teachers for their help.

<a id="w-1558"></a>
### 1558. [grave](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/grave1_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɡreɪv/` · **US:** `/ɡreɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gra/grave/grave__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gra/grave/grave__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**

  - The consequences will be very grave if nothing is done.
  - We were in grave danger.
  - I fear you are making a very grave mistake.

<a id="w-1564"></a>
### 1564. [guarantee](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/guarantee_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌɡærənˈtiː/` · **US:** `/ˌɡærənˈtiː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gua/guara/guarantee__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gua/guara/guarantee__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**

<a id="w-1565"></a>
### 1565. [guard](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/guard_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɡɑːd/` · **US:** `/ɡɑːrd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gua/guard/guard__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gua/guard/guard__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पहरेदार, बचाव, चौकीदार
- **Examples:**
  - prison/border guards
  - The building is protected by armed guards.
  - The prisoner slipped past the guards on the gate and escaped.
  - A guard was posted outside the building.

<a id="w-1566"></a>
### 1566. [guest](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/guest_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɡest/` · **US:** `/ɡest/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gue/guest/guest__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gue/guest/guest__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कुतुम्भ, अतिथि, मेहमान
- **Examples:**
  - They only use the dining room when they have guests.
  - She had invited six guests to dinner.
  - She felt that she had to entertain her guests.
  - There were more than 100 wedding guests.

<a id="w-1567"></a>
### 1567. [guidance](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/guidance) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɡaɪdns/` · **US:** `/ˈɡaɪdns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/gui/guida/guidance__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/gui/guida/guidance__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**

## Words 1,601 – 1,700

<a id="w-1601"></a>
### 1601. [hook](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/hook_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/hʊk/` · **US:** `/hʊk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hoo/hook_/hook__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hoo/hook_/hook__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** हुक, आँकड़ा, कुन्दा
- **Examples:**
  - a picture/curtain/coat hook
  - a fish hook
  - Hang your towel on the hook.
  - The key was hanging from a hook.

<a id="w-1602"></a>
### 1602. [horizon](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/horizon_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/həˈraɪzn/` · **US:** `/həˈraɪzn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hor/horiz/horizon__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hor/horiz/horizon__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सीमा, क्षितिज
- **Examples:**
  - The sun sank below the horizon.
  - A ship appeared on the horizon.

<a id="w-1603"></a>
### 1603. [horror](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/horror) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈhɒrə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈhɔːrər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hor/horro/horror__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hor/horro/horror__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** डर, दहशत, विभीषिका
- **Examples:**
  - The thought of being left alone filled her with horror.
  - in horror People watched in horror as the plane crashed to the ground.
  - with horror She realized with horror that somebody had broken into her house.
  - to somebody's horror To his horror, he realized that he couldn't escape (= it upset him very much).

<a id="w-1604"></a>
### 1604. [horse](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/horse_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/hɔːs/` · **US:** `/hɔːrs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hor/horse/horse__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hor/horse/horse__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कुदान घोड़ा, खिलौना घोड़ा, शालिहोत्र
- **Examples:**
  - to ride a horse
  - to get on a horse
  - He mounted his horse and rode off.
  - She's a keen horse rider.

<a id="w-1605"></a>
### 1605. [hostile](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/hostile) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈhɒstaɪl/` · **US:** `/ˈhɑːstaɪl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hos/hosti/hostile__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hos/hosti/hostile__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** शत्रुतापूर्ण, द्वेषी
- **Examples:**
  - The speaker got a hostile reception from the audience.
  - hostile to/towards somebody/something She was openly hostile towards her parents.

<a id="w-1606"></a>
### 1606. [household](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/household) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈhaʊshəʊld/` · **US:** `/ˈhaʊshəʊld/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hou/house/household__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hou/house/household__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** घर के सभी लोग, परिवार, घरेलु
- **Examples:**
  - Most households now own at least one car.
  - The average household spends more on housing than on food.
  - young people from low-income households
  - a one-parent/single-parent/two-parent household

<a id="w-1607"></a>
### 1607. [housing](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/housing) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈhaʊzɪŋ/` · **US:** `/ˈhaʊzɪŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hou/housi/housing__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hou/housi/housing__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** घर, गृह, घोड़े का साज़ सामान
- **Examples:**
  - rental/student housing
  - temporary/permanent housing
  - low-cost/cheap housing
  - There is an urgent need to build more affordable housing.

<a id="w-1609"></a>
### 1609. [hunger](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/hunger_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈhʌŋɡə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈhʌŋɡər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hun/hunge/hunger__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hun/hunge/hunger__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** तीव्र इच्छा, भूख
- **Examples:**
  - Around fifty people die of hunger every day in the camp.
  - The organization works to alleviate world hunger and disease.

<a id="w-1610"></a>
### 1610. [hunt](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/hunt_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/hʌnt/` · **US:** `/hʌnt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hun/hunt_/hunt__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hun/hunt_/hunt__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** शिकार, शिकारी दल, तलाश
- **Examples:**
  - The hunt is on for a suitable candidate.
  - hunt for somebody/something Hundreds have joined a police hunt for the missing teenager.
  - on the hunt for somebody/something They have been on the hunt for a new chief executive since July.
  - brides-to-be on the hunt for the perfect wedding dress

<a id="w-1611"></a>
### 1611. [hunter](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/hunter)
- [Oxford Learner's Dictionary Search](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/hunter)
- **Meaning:** शिकारी, खोजी

<a id="w-1612"></a>
### 1612. [hurry](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/hurry_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈhʌri/` · **US:** `/ˈhɜːri/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hur/hurry/hurry__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hur/hurry/hurry__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** हुत जल्दबाजी, जल्दी
- **Examples:**
  - Take your time—there's no hurry.
  - What's the hurry? The train doesn't leave for an hour.
  - in your hurry to do something In my hurry to leave, I forgot my passport.

<a id="w-1613"></a>
### 1613. [hurt](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/hurt_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/hɜːt/` · **US:** `/hɜːrt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hur/hurt_/hurt__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hur/hurt_/hurt__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** दर्द पहुँचाना, चोट, ज़ख़्मी
- **Examples:**
  - None of the passengers were badly hurt.
  - Steve didn't look seriously hurt.
  - Stop that or you'll get hurt!

<a id="w-1614"></a>
### 1614. [identical](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/identical) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/aɪˈdentɪkl/` · **US:** `/aɪˈdentɪkl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ide/ident/identical__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ide/ident/identical__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जैसा, वैसा ही, बिलकुल् वही
- **Examples:**
  - a row of identical houses
  - The two pictures are similar, although not identical.
  - identical to somebody/something Her dress is almost identical to mine.
  - identical with somebody/something The name on the ticket should be identical with the one in the passport.

<a id="w-1615"></a>
### 1615. [identify](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/identify) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/aɪˈdentɪfaɪ/` · **US:** `/aɪˈdentɪfaɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ide/ident/identify__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ide/ident/identify__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समझना, पहचानना, शिनाख्त  करना
- **Examples:**
  - identify somebody/something She was able to identify her attacker.
  - Passengers were asked to identify their own suitcases before they were put on the plane.
  - identify yourself Many of those arrested refused to identify themselves (= would not say who they were).
  - identify somebody/something as somebody/something The bodies were identified as those of two suspected drug dealers.

<a id="w-1616"></a>
### 1616. [ignore](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/ignore) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪɡˈnɔː(r)/` · **US:** `/ɪɡˈnɔːr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ign/ignor/ignore__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ign/ignor/ignore__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ध्यान न देना, अनसुनी करना, अनदेखा
- **Examples:**
  - He ignored all the ‘No Smoking’ signs and lit up a cigarette.
  - I made a suggestion but they chose to ignore it.
  - We cannot afford to ignore their advice.
  - to ignore a warning/a problem/an issue

<a id="w-1617"></a>
### 1617. [illegal](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/illegal_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈliːɡl/` · **US:** `/ɪˈliːɡl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ill/illeg/illegal__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ill/illeg/illegal__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अवैध, नियम विरुद्ध, अवैध,गैरकानूनी
- **Examples:**
  - illegal drugs/firearms/substances
  - She promised to crack down on illegal immigration.
  - illegal immigrants/aliens
  - His illegal activities landed him in jail several times.

<a id="w-1618"></a>
### 1618. [illustrate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/illustrate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪləstreɪt/` · **US:** `/ˈɪləstreɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ill/illus/illustrate__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ill/illus/illustrate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** व्याख्या करना, स्पष्ट  करना, स्पष्ट करना
- **Examples:**
  - illustrate something She illustrated her own books.
  - illustrate something with something His lecture was illustrated with photos taken during the expedition.

<a id="w-1619"></a>
### 1619. [illustration](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/illustration) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌɪləˈstreɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌɪləˈstreɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ill/illus/illustration__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ill/illus/illustration__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्पष्टीकरण, व्यख्या, उदाहरन
- **Examples:**
  - 50 full-colour illustrations
  - detailed illustrations of animals, birds and plants
  - illustration by somebody a volume of Chaucer with illustrations by Edward Burne-Jones
  - illustration for something He produced illustrations for children's books.

<a id="w-1620"></a>
### 1620. [immediate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/immediate) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈmiːdiət/` · **US:** `/ɪˈmiːdiət/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/imm/immed/immediate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/imm/immed/immediate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मूल, सन्निहित, तत्काल
- **Examples:**
  - an immediate reaction/response
  - to take immediate action
  - RAM stores information for immediate access.
  - This decision will have an immediate impact on students.

<a id="w-1621"></a>
### 1621. [immediately](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/immediately_1) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈmiːdiətli/` · **US:** `/ɪˈmiːdiətli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/imm/immed/immediately__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/imm/immed/immediately__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ठीक, उसी समय, तत्काल
- **Examples:**
  - She answered almost immediately.
  - They immediately began arguing.
  - The point of my question may not be immediately apparent.
  - It is immediately clear that he is out of his depth.

<a id="w-1622"></a>
### 1622. [immigrant](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/immigrant) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪmɪɡrənt/` · **US:** `/ˈɪmɪɡrənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/imm/immig/immigrant__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/imm/immig/immigrant__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अप्रवासी, देशांतर में बसनेवाला
- **Examples:**
  - immigrant from… immigrants from other European countries
  - immigrant to… immigrants to the United States
  - Immigrants and their descendants have played a vital role in building this nation.
  - The president vowed to continue deporting illegal immigrants.

<a id="w-1623"></a>
### 1623. [immune](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/immune) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈmjuːn/` · **US:** `/ɪˈmjuːn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/imm/immun/immune__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/imm/immun/immune__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उन्मुक्त
- **Examples:**
  - Adults are often immune to German measles.

<a id="w-1624"></a>
### 1624. [implement](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/implement_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪmplɪment/` · **US:** `/ˈɪmplɪment/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/imp/imple/implement__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/imp/imple/implement__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अनुबंध करना, कार्यान्वयन करना, अमल में लाना
- **Examples:**
  - to implement changes/decisions/policies/reforms
  - A new work programme for young people will be implemented.

<a id="w-1625"></a>
### 1625. [implication](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/implication) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌɪmplɪˈkeɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌɪmplɪˈkeɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/imp/impli/implication__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/imp/impli/implication__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उलझाव, उलझन, निहितार्थ
- **Examples:**
  - They failed to consider the wider implications of their actions.
  - implication (of something) for something The development of the site will have implications for the surrounding countryside.

<a id="w-1626"></a>
### 1626. [imply](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/imply) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪmˈplaɪ/` · **US:** `/ɪmˈplaɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/imp/imply/imply__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/imp/imply/imply__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समझना, सूचित करना, सूचित  करना
- **Examples:**
  - imply (that)… Are you implying (that) I am wrong?
  - I disliked the implied criticism in his voice.
  - imply something His silence seemed to imply agreement.
  - High-yield bonds, as the name implies, offer a high rate of interest.

<a id="w-1627"></a>
### 1627. [impose](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/impose) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪmˈpəʊz/` · **US:** `/ɪmˈpəʊz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/imp/impos/impose__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/imp/impos/impose__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लगाना, थोपना, लागू करना
- **Examples:**
  - impose something The UN Security Council imposed sanctions in 1992.
  - to impose a penalty/fine/sentence/ban
  - impose something on/upon something/somebody A new tax was imposed on fuel.

<a id="w-1628"></a>
### 1628. [impossible](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/impossible) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪmˈpɒsəbl/` · **US:** `/ɪmˈpɑːsəbl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/imp/impos/impossible__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/imp/impos/impossible__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** असम्भव
- **Examples:**
  - almost/virtually/nearly impossible
  - Only a year ago and this would have seemed impossible.
  - Getting any kind of decent job was proving impossible.
  - an impossible dream/goal

<a id="w-1629"></a>
### 1629. [impression](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/impression) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪmˈpreʃn/` · **US:** `/ɪmˈpreʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/imp/impre/impression__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/imp/impre/impression__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रभाव, दिखावा, धारणा
- **Examples:**
  - My first impression of him was favourable.
  - She gives the impression of being very busy.
  - impression that… I did not get the impression that they were unhappy about the situation.
  - I had the impression that it was all done in a hurry.

<a id="w-1630"></a>
### 1630. [impressive](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/impressive) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪmˈpresɪv/` · **US:** `/ɪmˈpresɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/imp/impre/impressive__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/imp/impre/impressive__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रभावशाली
- **Examples:**
  - an impressive performance
  - an impressive array/list/collection
  - an impressive display of skill
  - He has an impressive record of 63 wins from 71 fights.

<a id="w-1631"></a>
### 1631. [improve](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/improve_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪmˈpruːv/` · **US:** `/ɪmˈpruːv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/imp/impro/improve__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/imp/impro/improve__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बेहतर बनाना, सुधरना, सुधारना
- **Examples:**
  - Overall the situation has improved dramatically.
  - Things are improving every day.
  - You should see your score improve significantly.
  - Working conditions have greatly improved.

<a id="w-1632"></a>
### 1632. [improvement](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/improvement) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪmˈpruːvmənt/` · **US:** `/ɪmˈpruːvmənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/imp/impro/improvement__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/imp/impro/improvement__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सुधार, प्रगति, उन्नति
- **Examples:**
  - The economy has shown significant improvement over the past 9 months.
  - We expect to see further improvement over the coming year.
  - Sales figures continue to show signs of improvement.
  - There were areas that needed improvement.

<a id="w-1633"></a>
### 1633. [incident](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/incident_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪnsɪdənt/` · **US:** `/ˈɪnsɪdənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inc/incid/incident__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inc/incid/incident__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** वारदात, घट्, प्रसंग
- **Examples:**
  - His bad behaviour was just an isolated incident.
  - The most recent incident occurred last January.
  - She couldn't have been more than five years old when the incident happened.
  - One particular incident sticks in my mind.

<a id="w-1634"></a>
### 1634. [income](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/income) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪnkəm/` · **US:** `/ˈɪnkəm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inc/incom/income__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inc/incom/income__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** फ़ायदा, आय, कमाई
- **Examples:**
  - Average household income fell slightly.
  - a weekly disposable income (= the money that you have left to spend after tax, etc.) of £200
  - His parents have a combined annual income of less than $35  000.
  - Net income for the year was $43m.

<a id="w-1635"></a>
### 1635. [incorporate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/incorporate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈkɔːpəreɪt/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈkɔːrpəreɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inc/incor/incorporate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inc/incor/incorporate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समाविष्ट  करना, समाविष्ट करना, सम्मिलित करना
- **Examples:**
  - incorporate something The new car design incorporates all the latest safety features.
  - incorporate something in/into/within something We have incorporated all the latest safety features into the design.
  - Many of your suggestions have been incorporated in the plan.

<a id="w-1636"></a>
### 1636. [incredible](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/incredible) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈkredəbl/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈkredəbl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inc/incre/incredible__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inc/incre/incredible__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अविश्वसनीय, अच्छा, असाधारण
- **Examples:**
  - an incredible story
  - it is incredible that… It seemed incredible that she had been there a week already.
  - it is incredible to somebody that… It's just incredible to me that only one person was hurt.
  - it is incredible how… It really is incredible how she managed to get away with it.

<a id="w-1637"></a>
### 1637. [index](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/index_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪndeks/` · **US:** `/ˈɪndeks/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ind/index/index__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ind/index/index__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** तालिका, सूचक, सूची
- **Examples:**
  - Look it up in the index.
  - Author and subject indexes are available on a library database.

<a id="w-1638"></a>
### 1638. [indication](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/indication) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌɪndɪˈkeɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌɪndɪˈkeɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ind/indic/indication__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ind/indic/indication__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** संकेत, लक्षण, सुझाव
- **Examples:**
  - indication of something They gave no indication of how the work should be done.
  - indication of doing something He shows every indication (= clear signs) of wanting to accept the post.
  - indication that… There are clear indications that the economy is improving.
  - All the indications are that the deal will go ahead as planned.

<a id="w-1639"></a>
### 1639. [indoor](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/indoor) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪndɔː(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈɪndɔːr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ind/indoo/indoor__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ind/indoo/indoor__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भीतरी, आंतरिक, घर  का
- **Examples:**
  - an indoor swimming pool
  - indoor games
  - Poor indoor air quality can result in increased risk of asthma and allergies.

<a id="w-1640"></a>
### 1640. [infant](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/infant_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪnfənt/` · **US:** `/ˈɪnfənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inf/infan/infant__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inf/infan/infant__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बच्चा, लाल, शिशु
- **Examples:**
  - a nursery for infants under two
  - their infant son

<a id="w-1641"></a>
### 1641. [infect](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/infect) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈfekt/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈfekt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inf/infec/infect__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inf/infec/infect__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** इनफेक्ट करना, दूषित करना, प्रभावित करना
- **Examples:**
  - infect somebody/something (with something) It is not possible to infect another person through kissing.
  - (be) infected with something people infected with HIV

<a id="w-1642"></a>
### 1642. [infection](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/infection) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈfekʃn/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈfekʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inf/infec/infection__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inf/infec/infection__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्वर  परिवर्तन, संक्रामक रोग, छूत
- **Examples:**
  - to cause/prevent infection
  - Vaccination is essential to protect people exposed to hepatitis B infection.
  - The drugs slow down the progression of HIV infection.
  - Bacterial infection of the wound may occur.

<a id="w-1643"></a>
### 1643. [inform](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/inform) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈfɔːm/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈfɔːrm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inf/infor/inform__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inf/infor/inform__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बताना, सूचित करना, प्रेरित करना
- **Examples:**
  - inform somebody The government took this decision without consulting Parliament or informing the public.
  - Have the police been informed?
  - inform somebody of something Please inform us of any changes of address.
  - He went to inform them of his decision.

<a id="w-1644"></a>
### 1644. [infrastructure](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/infrastructure) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪnfrəstrʌktʃə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈɪnfrəstrʌktʃər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inf/infra/infrastructure__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inf/infra/infrastructure__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आधारभूत संरचना, आधारिक संरचना, आधारभूत  संरचना

<a id="w-1645"></a>
### 1645. [initial](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/initial_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈnɪʃl/` · **US:** `/ɪˈnɪʃl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ini/initi/initial__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ini/initi/initial__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रारंभिक, प्रारम्भिक, प्रथमाक्षर
- **Examples:**
  - There is an initial payment of £60 followed by ten instalments of £25.
  - The government will make an initial investment of $ 4.7 billion in the company.
  - in the initial stages (= at the beginning) of the campaign
  - an initial phase/period

<a id="w-1646"></a>
### 1646. [initially](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/initially) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈnɪʃəli/` · **US:** `/ɪˈnɪʃəli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ini/initi/initially__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ini/initi/initially__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** शुरू में
- **Examples:**
  - Initially, the system worked well.
  - More people had been infected than was initially thought.
  - He was initially reluctant to join the project.
  - The scientific work will be conducted, at least initially, at our laboratories.

<a id="w-1647"></a>
### 1647. [initiate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/initiate_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈnɪʃieɪt/` · **US:** `/ɪˈnɪʃieɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ini/initi/initiate__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ini/initi/initiate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आरम्भ करना, शुरू करना, बात उठाना
- **Examples:**
  - to initiate legal proceedings against somebody
  - The government has initiated a programme of economic reform.

<a id="w-1648"></a>
### 1648. [initiative](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/initiative) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪˈnɪʃətɪv/` · **US:** `/ɪˈnɪʃətɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ini/initi/initiative__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ini/initi/initiative__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अवसर, अगुआई, कदम
- **Examples:**
  - a United Nations peace initiative
  - Most policy initiatives come from the White House.
  - to launch an initiative
  - The money was intended to support initiatives in bilingual education.

<a id="w-1649"></a>
### 1649. [injure](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/injure) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪndʒə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈɪndʒər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inj/injur/injure__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inj/injur/injure__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** घायल, नुकसान पहुच्ना, ज़ख़्मी करना
- **Examples:**
  - He injured his knee playing hockey.
  - Three people were killed and five injured in the crash.
  - She injured herself during training.
  - She was seriously injured in a riding accident.

<a id="w-1650"></a>
### 1650. [injury](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/injury) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪndʒəri/` · **US:** `/ˈɪndʒəri/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inj/injur/injury__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inj/injur/injury__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** घाव, क्षति, आकस्मिक चोट
- **Examples:**
  - Two people sustained minor injuries.
  - One of the girls suffered serious injuries.
  - He was lucky to escape injury.
  - The passengers escaped with only minor injuries.

<a id="w-1651"></a>
### 1651. [inner](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/inner) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪnə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈɪnər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inn/inner/inner__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inn/inner/inner__us_1.mp3)
