  - inner London

<a id="w-1652"></a>
### 1652. [innocent](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/innocent_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪnəsnt/` · **US:** `/ˈɪnəsnt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inn/innoc/innocent__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inn/innoc/innocent__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निर्दोष, मासूम, बेकसूर
- **Examples:**
  - They have imprisoned an innocent man.
  - innocent of something She was found innocent of any crime.
  - innocent of doing something I was convinced that he was innocent of taking the money.
  - He was the innocent party (= person) in the dispute.

<a id="w-1653"></a>
### 1653. [innovation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/innovation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌɪnəˈveɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌɪnəˈveɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inn/innov/innovation__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inn/innov/innovation__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** नवीनीकरण, नवीनता, नवाचार
- **Examples:**
  - an age of technological innovation
  - The company is very interested in product design and innovation.
  - Too strict a regulatory system will stifle innovation.
  - innovation in something innovation in engineering

<a id="w-1654"></a>
### 1654. [input](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/input_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪnpʊt/` · **US:** `/ˈɪnpʊt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inp/input/input__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inp/input/input__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निवेश, सहयोग, कम्प्यूटर में भरी सामग्री
- **Examples:**
  - I'd appreciate your input on this.
  - input into something They all had some input into the discussion.
  - input to something Barley is one of the main inputs to the process of distillation.
  - input of something Growing anything in this soil will require heavy inputs of nutrients.

<a id="w-1655"></a>
### 1655. [inquiry](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/inquiry) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈkwaɪəri/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈkwaɪəri/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/enq/enqui/enquiry__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/enq/enqui/enquiry__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जाँच, सर्वेक्षण, अन्वेषण
- **Examples:**
  - a murder inquiry
  - inquiry into something a public inquiry into the environmental effects of the proposed new road
  - to hold/conduct/launch an inquiry into the affair

<a id="w-1656"></a>
### 1656. [insect](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/insect) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪnsekt/` · **US:** `/ˈɪnsekt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ins/insec/insect__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ins/insec/insect__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कीड़ा, कीट, तुच्छ आदमी
- **Examples:**
  - Most birds spend most of the day eating insects.
  - There are about 30 million species of insects in the world.
  - insect species
  - insect repellent (= a chemical that keeps insects away)

<a id="w-1657"></a>
### 1657. [insight](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/insight) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪnsaɪt/` · **US:** `/ˈɪnsaɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ins/insig/insight__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ins/insig/insight__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अन्तर्दृष्टि, अंत्ःदृर्टि पूर्ण विचार
- **Examples:**
  - There are many valuable insights in her book.
  - His work offers several useful insights.
  - insight into something The book gives us fascinating insights into life in Mexico.
  - I hope you have gained some insight into the difficulties we face.

<a id="w-1658"></a>
### 1658. [insist](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/insist) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈsɪst/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈsɪst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ins/insis/insist__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ins/insis/insist__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अड़े रहना, जोर डालना, दृढ़ रहना
- **Examples:**
  - I didn't really want to go but he insisted.
  - Stay and have lunch. I insist!
  - ‘Please come with us.’ ‘Very well then, if you insist.’

<a id="w-1659"></a>
### 1659. [inspect](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/inspect) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈspekt/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈspekt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ins/inspe/inspect__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ins/inspe/inspect__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** परीक्षण करना, जांच करना, निरीक्षण करना
- **Examples:**
  - inspect something/somebody The teacher walked around inspecting their work.
  - Make sure you inspect the goods before signing for them.
  - inspect something/somebody for something The plants are regularly inspected for disease.

<a id="w-1660"></a>
### 1660. [install](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/install) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈstɔːl/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈstɔːl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ins/insta/install__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ins/insta/install__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बैठाना, लगाना[यन्त्र], पदारुढ़ होना
- **Examples:**
  - They're planning to install a new drainage system.
  - Make sure the equipment is properly installed.
  - A hidden camera had been installed in the room.

<a id="w-1661"></a>
### 1661. [instance](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/instance_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪnstəns/` · **US:** `/ˈɪnstəns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ins/insta/instance__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ins/insta/instance__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अवस्था
- **Examples:**
  - instance of somebody/something The report highlights a number of instances of injustice.
  - instance of doing something He admitted two instances of taking money from the till.
  - instance of somebody/something doing something There have been several instances of that happening.
  - in an instance In most instances, there will be no need for further treatment.

<a id="w-1662"></a>
### 1662. [instant](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/instant_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪnstənt/` · **US:** `/ˈɪnstənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ins/insta/instant__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ins/insta/instant__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** तुरंत, क्षण, क्षणिक
- **Examples:**
  - This account gives you instant access to your money.
  - The show was an instant success.
  - People today seem to want instant gratification.

<a id="w-1663"></a>
### 1663. [instantly](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/instantly) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪnstəntli/` · **US:** `/ˈɪnstəntli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ins/insta/instantly__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ins/insta/instantly__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** तुरंत, तत्काल, शीघ्र
- **Examples:**
  - Her voice is instantly recognizable.
  - The driver was killed instantly.

<a id="w-1664"></a>
### 1664. [instead](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/instead) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈsted/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈsted/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ins/inste/instead__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ins/inste/instead__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उसके स्थान पर, बजाय, बदल में
- **Examples:**
  - Lee was ill so I went instead.
  - He didn't reply. Instead, he turned on his heel and left the room.
  - She said nothing, preferring instead to save her comments till later.
  - She might have pursued a successful career in business, but she chose instead to become a nun.

<a id="w-1665"></a>
### 1665. [instinct](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/instinct) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪnstɪŋkt/` · **US:** `/ˈɪnstɪŋkt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ins/insti/instinct__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ins/insti/instinct__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रकृति, प्रवृति, सुझ भुज
- **Examples:**
  - She did not seem to have any of the usual maternal instincts.
  - by instinct Children do not know by instinct the difference between right and wrong.
  - instinct (is) to do something His first instinct was to run away.
  - instinct for (doing) something Horses have a well-developed instinct for fear.

<a id="w-1666"></a>
### 1666. [institute](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/institute_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪnstɪtjuːt/` · **US:** `/ˈɪnstɪtuːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ins/insti/institute__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ins/insti/institute__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** संस्थान, संस्था भवन
- **Examples:**
  - The report was compiled by Germany's five leading economic research institutes.
  - institute of something The course is offered at six colleges and institutes of higher education.
  - He is a member of the Institute of Chartered Accountants.
  - at an institute She was a senior researcher at the institute.

<a id="w-1667"></a>
### 1667. [institution](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/institution) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌɪnstɪˈtjuːʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌɪnstɪˈtuːʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ins/insti/institution__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ins/insti/institution__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** संस्थान, संसथा भवन, संस्थापन
- **Examples:**
  - The deal is backed by one of the country's largest financial institutions.
  - He has worked as a visiting lecturer for various educational institutions.
  - The system is targeted mainly at academic and research institutions.
  - the Smithsonian Institution

<a id="w-1668"></a>
### 1668. [instruction](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/instruction_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈstrʌkʃn/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈstrʌkʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ins/instr/instruction__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ins/instr/instruction__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** शिक्षा, जानकारी, शिक्षण
- **Examples:**
  - Follow the instructions on the packet carefully.
  - Always read the instructions before you start.
  - Step-by-step instructions are provided.
  - instructions on something The plant comes with full instructions on how to care for it.

<a id="w-1669"></a>
### 1669. [instructor](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/instructor) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈstrʌktə(r)/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈstrʌktər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ins/instr/instructor__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ins/instr/instructor__us_4.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उपदेशक, प्रसिक्षकअ, अनुदेशक
- **Examples:**
  - a fitness/driving/ski instructor
  - a qualified/certified instructor
  - He worked for a time as an aerobics instructor.

<a id="w-1670"></a>
### 1670. [instrument](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/instrument) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪnstrəmənt/` · **US:** `/ˈɪnstrəmənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ins/instr/instrument__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ins/instr/instrument__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मानव यंत्र, प्रपत्र, लिखित
- **Examples:**
  - to play an instrument
  - Is he learning an instrument?
  - percussion/brass/string instruments
  - an instrument maker

<a id="w-1671"></a>
### 1671. [insurance](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/insurance) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈʃɔːrəns/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈʃʊrəns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ins/insur/insurance__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ins/insur/insurance__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सुरक्षा, बीमा क़िश्त, बीमा पालिसी
- **Examples:**
  - health/medical insurance
  - car/travel/unemployment insurance
  - Private insurance accounts for about 35 per cent of healthcare in the US.
  - to have/buy insurance

<a id="w-1672"></a>
### 1672. [intellectual](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/intellectual_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˌɪntəˈlektʃuəl/` · **US:** `/ˌɪntəˈlektʃuəl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/intel/intellectual__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/intel/intellectual__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बुद्धिगत, प्रज्ञावान
- **Examples:**
  - Gifted children typically show great intellectual curiosity and a wide range of interests.
  - an intellectual novel

<a id="w-1673"></a>
### 1673. [intelligence](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/intelligence) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈtelɪdʒəns/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈtelɪdʒəns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/intel/intelligence__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/intel/intelligence__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ज्ञान, गुप्त समाचार, बुद्धिमत्ता
- **Examples:**
  - a person of high/average/low intelligence
  - He didn't even have the intelligence to call for an ambulance.
  - As a director, he tends to underestimate the intelligence of his audience.
  - Please don't insult my intelligence by lying to me.

<a id="w-1674"></a>
### 1674. [intelligent](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/intelligent) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈtelɪdʒənt/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈtelɪdʒənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/intel/intelligent__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/intel/intelligent__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बुद्धिमान, सुधिरस, तरसेम
- **Examples:**
  - a highly intelligent child
  - She is clearly extremely intelligent.
  - to have an intelligent conversation/discussion/debate
  - to ask an intelligent question

<a id="w-1675"></a>
### 1675. [intend](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/intend) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈtend/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈtend/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/inten/intend__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/inten/intend__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** चाहना, विचार होना, रखना
- **Examples:**
  - We finished later than we had intended.
  - intend to do something I fully intended (= definitely intended) to pay for the damage.
  - I never intended to hurt you.
  - The train we had originally intended to catch had already left.

<a id="w-1676"></a>
### 1676. [intense](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/intense) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈtens/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈtens/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/inten/intense__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/inten/intense__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अत्यधिक, तीव्र, भावुक
- **Examples:**
  - We were all suffering in the intense heat.
  - intense cold/pain
  - They watched with intense interest.
  - intense pleasure/desire/anger

<a id="w-1677"></a>
### 1677. [intensity](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/intensity) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈtensəti/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈtensəti/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/inten/intensity__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/inten/intensity__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रबलता, उग्रता, प्रचंडता
- **Examples:**
  - intensity of light/sound/colour
  - intensity of feeling/concentration/relief
  - He was watching her with an intensity that was unnerving.
  - The storm resumed with even greater intensity.

<a id="w-1678"></a>
### 1678. [intention](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/intention) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈtenʃn/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈtenʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/inten/intention__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/inten/intention__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** धारणा, विचार, अंतिम लक्ष्य
- **Examples:**
  - intention of doing something I have no intention of going to the wedding.
  - I have every intention of paying her back what I owe her.
  - with the intention of doing something He left England with the intention of travelling in Africa.
  - intention to do something He has announced his intention to retire.

<a id="w-1679"></a>
### 1679. [interact](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/interact) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˌɪntərˈækt/` · **US:** `/ˌɪntərˈækt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/inter/interact__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/inter/interact__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बातचीत करना, एक दूसरे को प्रभावित करना
- **Examples:**
  - Teachers have a limited amount of time to interact with each child.
  - He interacts very well with other children.
  - The only thing he interacts with is his computer!

<a id="w-1680"></a>
### 1680. [interaction](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/interaction) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌɪntərˈækʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌɪntərˈækʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/inter/interaction__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/inter/interaction__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पारस्परिक प्रभाव, पारस्परिक क्रिया, पारस्परिक व्यवहार
- **Examples:**
  - the interaction between performers and their audience

<a id="w-1681"></a>
### 1681. [intermediate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/intermediate_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˌɪntəˈmiːdiət/` · **US:** `/ˌɪntərˈmiːdiət/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/inter/intermediate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/inter/intermediate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मध्यवर्ती, अंतःस्थ, अन्तःस्थायी
- **Examples:**
  - an intermediate stage/step in a process
  - intermediate between A and B Liquid crystals are considered to be intermediate between liquid and solid.

<a id="w-1682"></a>
### 1682. [internal](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/internal) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈtɜːnl/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈtɜːrnl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/inter/internal__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/inter/internal__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भीतरी, आंतरिक, गोपनीय
- **Examples:**
  - the internal structure of a building
  - internal doors

<a id="w-1683"></a>
### 1683. [interpret](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/interpret) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈtɜːprət/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈtɜːrprət/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/inter/interpret__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/inter/interpret__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अदा करना, अर्थ लगाना, वर्णन करना
- **Examples:**
  - The students were asked to interpret the poem.
  - The data can be interpreted in many different ways.
  - These results must be interpreted cautiously.

<a id="w-1684"></a>
### 1684. [interpretation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/interpretation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˌtɜːprəˈteɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ɪnˌtɜːrprəˈteɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/inter/interpretation__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/inter/interpretation__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्पष्टीकरण, व्याख्या, अर्थ
- **Examples:**
  - Her evidence suggests a different interpretation of the events.
  - It is not possible for everyone to put their own interpretation on the law.
  - Dreams are open to interpretation (= they can be explained in different ways).

<a id="w-1685"></a>
### 1685. [interrupt](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/interrupt) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˌɪntəˈrʌpt/` · **US:** `/ˌɪntəˈrʌpt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/inter/interrupt__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/inter/interrupt__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** रोकना, टोकना, रोक देना
- **Examples:**
  - Sorry to interrupt, but there's someone to see you.
  - interrupt with something Would you mind not interrupting with questions all the time?
  - interrupt somebody/something I hope I'm not interrupting you.
  - They were interrupted by a knock at the door.

<a id="w-1686"></a>
### 1686. [interval](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/interval) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪntəvl/` · **US:** `/ˈɪntərvl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/inter/interval__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/inter/interval__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विराम, अंतराल, अन्तराल
- **Examples:**
  - The interval between major earthquakes might be 200 years.
  - He knocked on the door and after a brief interval it was opened.

<a id="w-1687"></a>
### 1687. [intervene](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/intervene) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˌɪntəˈviːn/` · **US:** `/ˌɪntərˈviːn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/inter/intervene__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/inter/intervene__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** हस्तक्षेप करना, बीच मेँ आना, बीचबचाव करना , हस्तक्षेप करना
- **Examples:**
  - She might have been killed if the neighbours hadn't intervened.
  - intervene in something The President intervened personally in the crisis.
  - intervene between A and B She went over to intervene between the two men.
  - intervene against somebody They would not intervene against the rebels themselves.

<a id="w-1688"></a>
### 1688. [intervention](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/intervention) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌɪntəˈvenʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌɪntərˈvenʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/inter/intervention__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/inter/intervention__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** हस्तक्षेप, मध्यवर्त
- **Examples:**
  - calls for government intervention to save the steel industry
  - intervention in something In the second group of states, direct intervention in the economy was limited.

<a id="w-1689"></a>
### 1689. [interview](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/interview_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪntəvjuː/` · **US:** `/ˈɪntərvjuː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/inter/interview__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/inter/interview__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बातचीत, साक्षात्कार, साक्षत्कार
- **Examples:**
  - a job interview
  - She's been called for (an) interview.
  - interview for something He has an interview next week for the manager's job.
  - interview with somebody/something (for something) I got an interview with a PR company for a trainee position.

<a id="w-1690"></a>
### 1690. [intimate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/intimate_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪntɪmət/` · **US:** `/ˈɪntɪmət/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/intim/intimate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/intim/intimate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बताना, घनिष्ठ, आत्मीय
- **Examples:**
  - intimate friends
  - We're not on intimate terms with our neighbours.

<a id="w-1691"></a>
### 1691. [introduce](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/introduce) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˌɪntrəˈdjuːs/` · **US:** `/ˌɪntrəˈduːs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/intro/introduce__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/intro/introduce__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लगाना, प्रस्तुत करना, पेश करना
- **Examples:**
  - introduce somebody Allow me to introduce my mother.
  - The lead singer introduced each member of the band.
  - introduce somebody as something He introduced me as a new member of the company.
  - introduce A to B He introduced me to a Greek girl at the party.

<a id="w-1692"></a>
### 1692. [introduction](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/introduction) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌɪntrəˈdʌkʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌɪntrəˈdʌkʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/int/intro/introduction__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/int/intro/introduction__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रारंभ, समावेशन, शुरूआत
- **Examples:**
  - a book with an excellent introduction and notes
  - introduction to something Can you write a brief introduction to the text?
  - introduction by somebody The English edition has an introduction by Herbert Read.
  - in an introduction His mother is mentioned in the introduction.

<a id="w-1693"></a>
### 1693. [invade](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/invade) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈveɪd/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈveɪd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inv/invad/invade__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inv/invad/invade__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आक्रमण करना, चढाई करना, घुस जाना
- **Examples:**
  - Troops invaded on August 9th that year.
  - invade something When did the Romans invade Britain?

<a id="w-1694"></a>
### 1694. [invasion](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/invasion) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈveɪʒn/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈveɪʒn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inv/invas/invasion__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inv/invas/invasion__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** आक्रमण, हस्तक्षेप, धावा
- **Examples:**
  - the German invasion of Poland in 1939
  - the threat of invasion
  - an invasion force/fleet

<a id="w-1695"></a>
### 1695. [invent](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/invent) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈvent/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈvent/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inv/inven/invent__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inv/inven/invent__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बनाना, आविष्कार करना, गढना
- **Examples:**
  - Who invented the steam engine?
  - I wish mobile phones had never been invented!
  - The term ‘sociology’ was invented by Auguste Comte.

<a id="w-1696"></a>
### 1696. [invention](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/invention) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈvenʃn/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈvenʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inv/inven/invention__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inv/inven/invention__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रतिभा, आविष्कार, काल्पनिक कथा
- **Examples:**
  - Fax machines were a wonderful invention at the time.
  - He failed to patent his invention and never made a penny from it.

<a id="w-1697"></a>
### 1697. [invest](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/invest) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈvest/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈvest/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inv/inves/invest__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inv/inves/invest__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** धन लगाना, लगाना, देना
- **Examples:**
  - Now is a good time to invest.
  - invest in something She advised us to invest in the property market.
  - invest something If you want to invest your money, you are probably better off with bonds.
  - invest something in something He invested his life savings in his daughter's business.

<a id="w-1698"></a>
### 1698. [investigate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/investigate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈvestɪɡeɪt/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈvestɪɡeɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inv/inves/investigate__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inv/inves/investigate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पता लगाना, अनुसंधान करना, सोच विचार करना
- **Examples:**
  - The FBI has been called in to investigate.
  - the senior investigating officer
  - (informal) ‘What was that noise?’ ‘I'll go and investigate.’
  - investigate something Detectives are currently investigating possible links between the murders.

<a id="w-1699"></a>
### 1699. [investigation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/investigation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˌvestɪˈɡeɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ɪnˌvestɪˈɡeɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inv/inves/investigation__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inv/inves/investigation__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अनुसन्धान, जाँच, अन्वेषण
- **Examples:**
  - a criminal/murder/police investigation
  - an accident investigation team
  - The president has promised a full investigation.
  - The authorities have launched an investigation to determine why the ferry sank.

<a id="w-1700"></a>
### 1700. [investigator](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/investigator) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈvestɪɡeɪtə(r)/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈvestɪɡeɪtər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inv/inves/investigator__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inv/inves/investigator__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** जाँच करने वाला, जाँच कर्ता, गवेक्षक
- **Examples:**
  - air safety investigators
  - a private investigator (= a detective)

## Words 1,701 – 1,800

<a id="w-1701"></a>
### 1701. [investment](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/investment) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈvestmənt/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈvestmənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inv/inves/investment__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inv/inves/investment__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निवेशन, निवेश, विनीयुक्त धन
- **Examples:**
  - foreign/private investment
  - capital/business/property investment
  - to attract/encourage investment
  - investment in something This country needs investment in education.

<a id="w-1702"></a>
### 1702. [investor](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/investor) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈvestə(r)/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈvestər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inv/inves/investor__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inv/inves/investor__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-1703"></a>
### 1703. [invisible](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/invisible) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈvɪzəbl/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈvɪzəbl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inv/invis/invisible__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inv/invis/invisible__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अलक्ष्य, अप्रकट, अदृश्य
- **Examples:**
  - a wizard who could make himself invisible
  - She felt invisible in the crowd.
  - invisible to somebody/something stars invisible to the naked eye

<a id="w-1704"></a>
### 1704. [invitation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/invitation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌɪnvɪˈteɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌɪnvɪˈteɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inv/invit/invitation__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inv/invit/invitation__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** संदेश पत्र, दावत, उपहार
- **Examples:**
  - to issue/extend an invitation
  - to accept/turn down/decline an invitation
  - invitation to something I received an invitation to the party
  - invitation for something She repeated her invitation for dinner at her place.

<a id="w-1705"></a>
### 1705. [invite](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/invite_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈvaɪt/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈvaɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inv/invit/invite__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inv/invit/invite__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उकसाना, बुलाना, आमंट्रित करना
- **Examples:**
  - invite somebody to something Have you been invited to their party?
  - She very kindly invited me to lunch.
  - invite somebody I'd have liked to have gone but I wasn't invited.
  - My mother invited a few friends that she hadn't seen for 20 years.

<a id="w-1706"></a>
### 1706. [invoke](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/invoke) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈvəʊk/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈvəʊk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inv/invok/invoke__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inv/invok/invoke__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बुलाना, आह्वान करना, उपयोग करना
- **Examples:**
  - It is unlikely that libel laws will be invoked.

### 1707. [ironic](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/ironic) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/aɪˈrɒnɪk/` · **US:** `/aɪˈrɑːnɪk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/iro/ironi/ironic__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/iro/ironi/ironic__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** व्यंग्यात्मक, व्यंग्यपूर्ण, विडंबनात्मक
- **Examples:**
  - an ironic comment

<a id="w-1708"></a>
### 1708. [irony](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/irony) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈaɪrəni/` · **US:** `/ˈaɪrəni/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/iro/irony/irony__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/iro/irony/irony__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** व्यंग्योक्ति, व्याजोक्ति
- **Examples:**
  - It was one of life's little ironies.
  - the irony (is) that… The irony is that when he finally got the job, he discovered he didn't like it.

<a id="w-1709"></a>
### 1709. [isolate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/isolate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈaɪsəleɪt/` · **US:** `/ˈaɪsəleɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/iso/isola/isolate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/iso/isola/isolate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** समाधान करना, अलग रखना, अलग रख् करना
- **Examples:**
  - isolate somebody/yourself/something Patients with the disease should be isolated.
  - Engineers isolated the gas supply to the house.
  - isolate somebody/yourself/something from somebody/something He was immediately isolated from the other prisoners.
  - This decision will isolate the country from the rest of Europe.

<a id="w-1710"></a>
### 1710. [isolation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/isolation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌaɪsəˈleɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌaɪsəˈleɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/iso/isola/isolation__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/iso/isola/isolation__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सितारा मछली, एकांत, अलगाव
- **Examples:**
  - geographical isolation
  - an isolation hospital/ward (= for people with infectious diseases)
  - isolation (from somebody/something) The country has been threatened with complete isolation from the international community unless the atrocities stop.
  - He lives in splendid isolation (= far from, or in a superior position to, everyone else).

<a id="w-1711"></a>
### 1711. [item](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/item) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈaɪtəm/` · **US:** `/ˈaɪtəm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ite/item_/item__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ite/item_/item__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अंश, विषय, समाचार
- **Examples:**

  - There are plenty of vegetarian items on the menu.
  - She still has five items on her to-do list.
  - Students had to select three topics from the list of items.

- **UK:** `/dʒeɪl/` · **US:** `/dʒeɪl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/j/jai/jail_/jail__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/j/jai/jail_/jail__us_1.mp3)

- **UK:** `/dʒɔɪnt/` · **US:** `/dʒɔɪnt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/j/joi/joint/joint__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/j/joi/joint/joint__us_1.mp3)

  - The report was a joint effort (= we worked on it together).

<a id="w-1729"></a>
### 1729. [justify](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/justify) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈdʒʌstɪfaɪ/` · **US:** `/ˈdʒʌstɪfaɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/j/jus/justi/justify__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/j/jus/justi/justify__us_2.mp3)

- **Examples:**

  - justify something Her success had justified the faith her teachers had put in her.

  - in length The river is 300 miles in length.
  - The snake usually reaches a length of 100 cm.

<a id="w-1793"></a>
### 1793. [lung](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/lung) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/lʌŋ/` · **US:** `/lʌŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/lun/lung_/lung__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/lun/lung_/lung__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**

  - The storm is expected to move north today. Concerns are mounting meanwhile for a missing surfer.

### 1870. [minority](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/minority) *(noun)*

- **UK:** `/ˈməʊtɪv/` · **US:** `/ˈməʊtɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/mot/motiv/motive__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/mot/motiv/motive__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - Journalists are supposed to be politically neutral.
  - I didn't take my father's or my mother's side; I tried to remain neutral.

### 1939. [nod](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/nod_1) *(verb)*

### 1941. [nonetheless](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/nonetheless) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˌnʌnðəˈles/` · **US:** `/ˌnʌnðəˈles/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/non/nonet/nonetheless__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/non/nonet/nonetheless__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** फिर भी, तब भी

<a id="w-1942"></a>
### 1942. [nonsense](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/nonsense) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈnɒnsns/` · **US:** `/ˈnɑːnsns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/non/nonse/nonsense__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/non/nonse/nonsense__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बकवास, निरर्थक, बेतुका
- **Examples:**
  - Reports that he has resigned are nonsense.
  - You're talking nonsense!
  - ‘I won't go.’ ‘Nonsense! You must go!’
  - It's nonsense to say they don't care.

<a id="w-1943"></a>
### 1943. [normal](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/normal_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈnɔːml/` · **US:** `/ˈnɔːrml/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/nor/norma/normal__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/nor/norma/normal__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सितारा मछली, साधारण, सामान्य
- **Examples:**
  - quite/perfectly (= completely) normal
  - Her temperature is normal.
  - They are just quiet, normal people.
  - it is normal (for somebody) to do something It's normal to feel tired after such a long trip.

<a id="w-1944"></a>
### 1944. [normally](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/normally) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈnɔːməli/` · **US:** `/ˈnɔːrməli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/nor/norma/normally__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/nor/norma/normally__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सामान्यतः, आसानी से, सामान्य रुपे  से
- **Examples:**
  - I would never normally discuss this.
  - He didn't eat as much as he normally does.
  - It normally takes 20 minutes to get there.
  - The track is normally used by farmers and their goats.

<a id="w-1945"></a>
### 1945. [notable](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/notable_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈnəʊtəbl/` · **US:** `/ˈnəʊtəbl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/not/notab/notable__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/not/notab/notable__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** महत्वपूर्ण, उल्लेखनीय, प्रसिद्ध
- **Examples:**
  - a notable success/achievement/example
  - His eyes are his most notable feature.
  - notable (for something) The town is notable for its ancient harbour.
  - With a few notable exceptions, everyone gave something.

<a id="w-1946"></a>
### 1946. [notably](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/notably) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈnəʊtəbli/` · **US:** `/ˈnəʊtəbli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/not/notab/notably__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/not/notab/notably__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विशेषकर, विशेषतः
- **Examples:**
  - The house had many drawbacks, most notably its price.
  - This argument has been put forward by many politicians, most notably by the Prime Minister and the Foreign Secretary.

<a id="w-1947"></a>
### 1947. [notion](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/notion) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈnəʊʃn/` · **US:** `/ˈnəʊʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/not/notio/notion__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/not/notio/notion__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** इच्छा, धारणा, विचार
- **Examples:**
  - notion of something a political system based on the notions of equality and liberty
  - She had only a vague notion of what might happen.
  - He has no notion of the difficulty of the problem.
  - The author challenges preconceived notions of storytelling.

<a id="w-1948"></a>
### 1948. [novel](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/novel_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈnɒvl/` · **US:** `/ˈnɑːvl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/nov/novel/novel__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/nov/novel/novel__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** नया, उपन्यास, अनूठा
- **Examples:**
  - a novel feature
  - Job-sharing is still a novel concept and it will take a while for employers to get used to it.
  - The plan sounded rather novel.
  - It was an American who came up with the novel idea of drive-in restaurants.

<a id="w-1949"></a>
### 1949. [nowhere](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/nowhere) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈnəʊweə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈnəʊwer/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/now/nowhe/nowhere__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/now/nowhe/nowhere__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कहीं नहीं
- **Examples:**
  - I had nowhere to live, so I was sleeping on my sister's couch.
  - There was nowhere for me to sit.
  - ‘Where are you going this weekend?’ ‘Nowhere special.’
  - She doesn't want to stay there but she has nowhere else to go.

### 1950. [nuclear](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/nuclear) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈnjuːkliə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈnuːkliər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/nuc/nucle/nuclear__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/nuc/nucle/nuclear__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-1952"></a>

- **UK:** `/ˈnjuːmərəs/` · **US:** `/ˈnuːmərəs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/num/numer/numerous__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/num/numer/numerous__us_1.mp3)

- **UK:** `/nʌt/` · **US:** `/nʌt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/nut/nut__/nut__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/nut/nut__/nut__us_1.mp3)

- **UK:** `/njuˈtrɪʃn/` · **US:** `/nuˈtrɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/nut/nutri/nutrition__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/nut/nutri/nutrition__us_1.mp3)

<a id="w-1958"></a>
### 1958. [obey](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/obey) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈbeɪ/` · **US:** `/əˈbeɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/obe/obey_/obey__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/obe/obey_/obey__us_2_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पालन करना, निभाना, कहना  मानना
- **Examples:**
  - obey something to obey a command/an order/rules/the law
  - He was arrested when he failed to obey a police instruction to stop.
  - He has turned into an efficient soldier, blindly obeying orders (= without questioning them).
  - obey somebody He had always obeyed his parents without question.

<a id="w-1959"></a>
### 1959. [objective](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/objective_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/əbˈdʒektɪv/` · **US:** `/əbˈdʒektɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/obj/objec/objective__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/obj/objec/objective__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निष्पक्ष, विषयनिष्ट
- **Examples:**
  - an objective assessment of the situation
  - objective truth/facts/reality
  - objective criteria/measures/measurements
  - He doesn't even pretend to be impartial and objective.

<a id="w-1960"></a>
### 1960. [obligation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/obligation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌɒblɪˈɡeɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌɑːblɪˈɡeɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/obl/oblig/obligation__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/obl/oblig/obligation__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अनुबंध पट्र, एहसान, आभार
- **Examples:**
  - obligation to do something You are under no obligation to buy anything.
  - under any/no obligation She did not feel under any obligation to tell him the truth.
  - I don't want people coming to see me out of a sense of obligation.
  - without obligation We will send you an estimate for the work without obligation (= you do not have to accept it).

<a id="w-1961"></a>
### 1961. [oblige](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/oblige) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈblaɪdʒ/` · **US:** `/əˈblaɪdʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/obl/oblig/oblige__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/obl/oblig/oblige__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** नैतिक नियमों से मज़बूर करना, काम करना, सहायता देना
- **Examples:**
  - Parents are obliged by law to send their children to school.
  - I felt obliged to ask them to dinner.
  - He suffered a serious injury that obliged him to give up work.
  - Libel plaintiffs are virtually obliged to go into the witness box.

<a id="w-1962"></a>
### 1962. [observation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/observation) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌɒbzəˈveɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌɑːbzərˈveɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/obs/obser/observation__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/obs/obser/observation__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विचार, कथन, देखरेख
- **Examples:**
  - Most information was collected by direct observation of the animals' behaviour.
  - results based on scientific observations
  - We managed to escape observation (= we were not seen).
  - under observation The suspect is being kept under observation (= watched closely by the police).

<a id="w-1963"></a>
### 1963. [observe](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/observe) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əbˈzɜːv/` · **US:** `/əbˈzɜːrv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/obs/obser/observe__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/obs/obser/observe__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अनुपालन करना, कहना, देखना
- **Examples:**
  - observe somebody/something Have you observed any changes lately?
  - All the characters in the novel are closely observed (= seem like people in real life).
  - I want you to observe all the details.
  - The patterns observed in our study may represent a general rule.

<a id="w-1964"></a>
### 1964. [observer](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/observer) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/əbˈzɜːvə(r)/` · **US:** `/əbˈzɜːrvər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/obs/obser/observer__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/obs/obser/observer__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रेक्षक, पर्यवेक्षक, परिदर्शक
- **Examples:**
  - According to observers, the plane exploded shortly after take-off.
  - To the casual observer (= somebody who does not pay much attention), the system appears confusing.
  - Most art forms require a contribution from the observer.

<a id="w-1965"></a>
### 1965. [obstacle](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/obstacle) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɒbstəkl/` · **US:** `/ˈɑːbstəkl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/obs/obsta/obstacle__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/obs/obsta/obstacle__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अवरोध, रुकावट, अवरोध/अटकाव
- **Examples:**
  - So far, we have managed to overcome all the obstacles that have been placed in our path.
  - obstacle to doing something A lack of qualifications can be a major obstacle to finding a job.
  - obstacle to somebody doing something The MP claims that there is now no obstacle to him standing at the next general election.
  - obstacle to something The huge distances involved have proved an obstacle to communication between villages.

<a id="w-1966"></a>
### 1966. [obtain](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/obtain) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əbˈteɪn/` · **US:** `/əbˈteɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/obt/obtai/obtain__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/obt/obtai/obtain__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पाना, प्राप्त करना, हाशिल करना
- **Examples:**
  - to obtain information/data/results
  - I've been trying to obtain permission to publish this material.
  - I finally managed to obtain a copy of the report.
  - Further details can be obtained by writing to the above address.

<a id="w-1967"></a>
### 1967. [obvious](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/obvious) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɒbviəs/` · **US:** `/ˈɑːbviəs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/obv/obvio/obvious__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/obv/obvio/obvious__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सुस्पष्ट, स्पष्ट, साक्षात्
- **Examples:**
  - I know you don't like her but try not to make it so obvious.
  - He agreed with obvious pleasure.
  - obvious (that…) It soon became obvious that the machine did not work.
  - It's painfully obvious that the budget was low for this movie.

<a id="w-1968"></a>
### 1968. [obviously](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/obviously) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɒbviəsli/` · **US:** `/ˈɑːbviəsli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/obv/obvio/obviously__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/obv/obvio/obviously__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्पष्ट रुप से, स्पष्टः
- **Examples:**
  - Obviously, we don't want to spend too much money.
  - Diet and exercise are obviously important.
  - I wanted to do well, obviously, but I wasn't nervous because I don't see exams as the be all and end all.
  - She responded 'Oh well, obviously I disagree with him'.

<a id="w-1969"></a>
### 1969. [occasion](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/occasion_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/əˈkeɪʒn/` · **US:** `/əˈkeɪʒn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/occ/occas/occasion__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/occ/occas/occasion__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अवसर, मौका, कारण
- **Examples:**
  - on an occasion on this/that occasion
  - I've met him on several occasions.
  - They have been seen together on two separate occasions.
  - On one occasion, she called me in the middle of the night.

<a id="w-1970"></a>
### 1970. [occasional](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/occasional) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/əˈkeɪʒənl/` · **US:** `/əˈkeɪʒənl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/occ/occas/occasional__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/occ/occas/occasional__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रासंगिक, असाम्यिक
- **Examples:**
  - He works for us on an occasional basis.
  - The occasional sweet treat won't do you any harm.
  - He spent five years in Paris, with occasional visits to Italy.
  - an occasional smoker (= a person who smokes, but not often)

<a id="w-1971"></a>
### 1971. [occasionally](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/occasionally) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈkeɪʒnəli/` · **US:** `/əˈkeɪʒnəli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/occ/occas/occasionally__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/occ/occas/occasionally__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** कभीकभी
- **Examples:**
  - We occasionally meet for a drink after work.
  - This type of allergy can very occasionally be fatal.
  - Only occasionally was there any doubt that they would succeed.
  - Johnston is an intelligent, occasionally brilliant writer.

- **UK:** `/ɒdz/` · **US:** `/ɑːdz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/odd/odds_/odds__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/odd/odds_/odds__us_1.mp3)
